## 新投稿 (108)

<h3 id="1">[1] How Far Did They Go? The Persuasive Tactics of Covert LLM Agents in a Discontinued Field Experiment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05256)
- **Authors**: Kokil Jaidka, Saifuddin Ahmed
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Persuasion, LLM Agent, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 该论文分析了被叫停的野外实验中未公开的LLM代理在Reddit辩论中使用的说服策略。通过结构化内容分析，发现代理大量使用身份定位、权威声明和认知偏差触发，与人类辩论者模式相反。结果表明需要审计框架来评估AI系统如何构建可信度，而非仅仅检测其存在。

<h3 id="2">[2] What Should Agents Say? Action-state Communication for Efficient Multi-Agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05304](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05304)
- **Authors**: Chen Huang, Yuhao Wu, Wenxuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, Agent Communication Protocol, Token Compression
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iNLP-Lab/PACT)
- **Summary**: 多Agent系统中自由文本通信导致令牌消耗大，影响性能和成本。论文提出PACT协议，将通信视为公共状态更新问题，压缩原始输出为动作状态记录。实验表明PACT在不同拓扑下一致改善性能-成本权衡，在SWE-agent上减少一半输入令牌。

<h3 id="3">[3] I Know What You Meme, Even If it Emerged Today: Understanding Evolving Memes through Open-World Knowledge Acquisition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05316)
- **Authors**: Shanhong Liu, Rui Cao, Pai Chet Ng, De Wen Soh
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Zero-Shot Learning, Knowledge Retrieval
- **Summary**: 多模态模因理解需要更新背景知识，现有方法依赖固定参数知识，无法处理新兴模因。论文提出Query-Retrieve-Conclude零样本框架，通过检索开放网络证据并合成背景知识来增强理解。在多个数据集上提升了知识恢复和模因检测性能。

<h3 id="4">[4] GITCO: Gated Inference-Time Context Optimization in TSFMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05332](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05332)
- **Authors**: Manya Pandey, Dhruv Kumar, Murari Mandal, Saurabh Deshpande
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, Inference Optimization, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 基于补丁的时间序列基础模型受上下文中毒影响，异常补丁降低预测质量。论文提出GITCO，通过门控机制在推理时优化输入上下文，无需参数更新。在TimesFM 2.5上平均MASE降低1.95%，达到改善上限的89.9%。

<h3 id="5">[5] Uncertainty Aware Functional Behavior Prediction and Material Fatigue Assessment for Circular Factory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05334](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05334)
- **Authors**: Nehal Afifi, Mehdi Khabou, Victor Mas, Jonas Hemmerich, Patric Grauberger, Stefan Dietrich, Volker Schulze, Sven Matthiesen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Prognostics and Health Management, Uncertainty Estimation, Industrial Monitoring
- **Summary**: 循环工厂中退货产品状态异构，现有PHM方法不能结合系统级功能预后和材料疲劳评估。论文提出结合不确定性感知功能预测和组件级疲劳评估的可靠性框架。在角磨机上测试，9个输出平均2%容差精度0.9652，驱动电机电流R²=0.9750。

<h3 id="6">[6] SentinelBench: A Benchmark for Long-Running Monitoring Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05342](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05342)
- **Authors**: Matheus Kunzler Maldaner, Adam Fourney, Amanda Swearngin, Hussein Mozzanar, Gagan Bansal, Maya Murad, Rafah Hosn, Saleema Amershi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Agent Evaluation, Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 长期运行任务需要持续监测而非连续行动，现有Agent评估缺乏此类基准。论文提出SentinelBench，包含100个时间演化监测任务，测量任务完成、反应时间和资源使用。在多个模型上建立基线，显示Agent设计选择影响关键指标。

<h3 id="7">[7] An interpretable and trustworthy AI framework for large-scale longitudinal structure-pain association studies using data from the Osteoarthritis Initiative (OAI)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05357)
- **Authors**: Jincheng Yu, Haoyang Li, Yiwen Liu, Shen Liu, Rachel Yuanbao Chen, C. Kent Kwoh, Hongxu Ding, Xiaoxiao Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Interpretability, Conformal Prediction
- **Summary**: 研究结构-疼痛关联需要大规模分析，但手动评分昂贵且深度学习预测不可靠。论文开发可解释AI框架，结合深度学习预测MOAKS评分和共形预测量化不确定性，再使用纵向潜在类别混合模型分析。MCC显著提升，并识别出快速和稳定进展轨迹。

<h3 id="8">[8] Synthetic Contrastive Reasoning for Multi-Table Q&A</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05382](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05382)
- **Authors**: Ankit Pratap Singh, Xin Su, Phillip Howard
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Table Reasoning, Question Answering, Preference Learning
- **Summary**: 多表问答缺乏推理监督数据。论文构建合成对比推理轨迹数据集，使用对比偏好优化微调LLM。在Qwen3-14B等模型上，CPO比SFT提升9.7%-16.3%，生成轨迹忠实且对比有意义。

<h3 id="9">[9] Stability vs. Manipulability: Evaluating Robustness Under Post-Decision Interaction in LLM Judges</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05384](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05384)
- **Authors**: Srimonti Dutta, Akshata Kishore Moharir
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Robustness Evaluation, LLM Safety
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 GEM Workshop
- **Summary**: LLM-as-judge评估假定判断稳定，但交互后可能被操纵。论文研究后决策可操纵性，发现LLM法官在针对性挑战后判断可逆转，权威框架影响大。提出评估鲁棒性分数ERS，建议评估协议需考虑交互鲁棒性。

<h3 id="10">[10] Residual Modeling for High-Fidelity Learned Compression of Scientific Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05389](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05389)
- **Authors**: Liangji Zhu, Sanjay Ranka, Anand Rangarajan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Neural Compression, Scientific Computing, Model Compression
- **Summary**: 科学数据有损压缩在高保真度下残差校正编码率增长快。论文提出残差编码器LBRC和NGLR，结合自适应量化和因果神经预测，降低残差熵。在NRMSE 10⁻⁶到10⁻⁴下，LBRC比GAE提高压缩比30-60%，NGLR再提高10-40%。

<h3 id="11">[11] LeanMarathon: Toward Reliable AI Co-Mathematicians through Long-Horizon Lean Autoformalization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05400](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05400)
- **Authors**: Yuanhe Zhang, Yuekai Sun, Taiji Suzuki, Jason D. Lee, Fanghui Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Mathematical Reasoning, Multi-Agent System
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YuanheZ/LeanMarathon)
- **Summary**: 长周期数学自动形式化中语句漂移、依赖缠结，失败率高。论文提出LeanMarathon多Agent框架，以演化蓝图为核心，通过四类Agent协调和两阶段编排实现可靠形式化。在四个Erdős问题上形式化7个定理，证明258个引理和定理，无sorry。

<h3 id="12">[12] Harnessing Generalist Agents for Contextualized Time Series</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05404](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05404)
- **Authors**: Zihao Li, Kaifeng Jin, Yuanchen Bei, Jiaru Zou, Avaneesh Kumar, Xuying Ning, Yanjun Zhao, Mengting Ai, Baoyu Jing, Hanghang Tong, Jingrui He
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Time Series Forecasting, LLM Agent, Multimodal Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/iDEA-iSAIL-Lab-UIUC/TimeClaw)
- **Summary**: 时间序列通常有丰富上下文，但通用AI代理主要在文本空间，未与结构化时序信号对齐。论文提出TimeClaw，为LLM代理提供时序原生运行时支持，包括可执行工具、经验驱动能力演进和情景多模态记忆。在多个领域基准上性能提升。

<h3 id="13">[13] Agents' Last Exam</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05405](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05405)
- **Authors**: Yiyou Sun, Xinyang Han, Weichen Zhang, Yuanbo Pang, Tianyu Wang, Yuhan Cao, Yixiao Huang, Chris Duroiu, Haoyun Zhang, Jeffrey Lin, Weishu Zhang, Tyler Zeng, Ying Yan, Bo Liu, Hanson Wen, Mingyang Xu, Xiaoyuan Liu, Zimeng Chen, Weiyan Shi, Amanda Dsouza, Vincent Sunn Chen, Patrick Bryant, Carl Boettiger, Yamini Rangan, Bradley Rothenberg, Kyle Steinfeld, Arvind Rao, Tapio Schneider, Georgios Yannakakis, Laure Zanna, Kaan Ozbay, Ida Sim, Tarek Zohdi, George Em Karniadakis, Jack Gallant, Teresa Head-gordon, Yushan Li, Wenxi Deng, Tao Sun, Huiqi Wang, Zhun Wang, Justin Xu, Chris Yuhao Liu, Yafei Cheng, Rongwang Hu, Aras Bacho, Shengcao Cao, Zengyi Qin, Yixiong Chen, Hengduan Fan, Hao Liu, Lin Zeng, Shashank Muralidhar Bharadwaj, Litian Gong, Yingxuan Yang, Maojia Song, Ruheng Wang, Zongzheng Zhang, Honglin Bao, Shuo Lu, Jianhong Tu, Zhonghua Wang, Zheng Zhang, Zijiao Chen, yanqiong Jiang, Zhendong Li, Bohan Lyu, Chang Ma, Peiran Xu, Benran Zhang, Shangding Gu, Haoyue Hua, Haoyang Li, Wanzhe Liao, Chengzhi Liu, Junbo Peng, Haoran Sun, Zechen Xu, Bo Chen, Jiayi Cheng, Yi Jiang, Keying Kuang, Yuan Li, Youbang Pan, Ziyan Rao, Alexander Schubert, Yifan Shen, Vincent Siu, Xiatao Sun, Kangqi Zhang, Xiaopan Zhang, Yuchen Zhu, Ishaan Singh Chandok, Lei Ding, Jingxuan Fan, Andrew Glover, Jiaming Hu, Yiran Hu, Wenbo Huang, Zixin Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Agent Evaluation, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/rdi-berkeley/agents-last-exam)
- **Summary**: 现有基准未能衡量AI在经济上有价值的工作流程中的表现。论文引入Agents' Last Exam (ALE)，与250+专家合作覆盖13个行业集群1000+任务。当前最难层级完全通过率仅2.6%，旨在缩小基准成功与GDP相关影响之间的差距。

<h3 id="14">[14] Mutation Without Variation: Convergence Dynamics in LLM-Driven Program Evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05408](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05408)
- **Authors**: Can Gurkan, Forrest Stonedahl, Uri Wilensky
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Genetic Programming, Program Synthesis, LLM Application
- **Venue**: GECCO '26 Workshop on Large Language Models for and with Evolutionary Computation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/can-gurkan/lmca)
- **Summary**: LLM多次变异程序时，探索新形式还是重复旧形式？论文分析LLM驱动变异链，发现87%链中超过93%变异访问先前结构，短周期和自环主导，而经典GP变异不收敛。表明LLM变异存在系统性结构同质化偏差。

<h3 id="15">[15] A Motivational Architecture for Conversational AGI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05411](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05411)
- **Authors**: Anna Mikeda, Ben Goertzel
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Cognitive Architecture, Dialogue System, AGI Architecture
- **Venue**: AGI-26
- **Summary**: 对话Agent的动机架构通常基于物理Agent，不适用于语言环境。论文提出对话OpenPsi动机架构，结合MetaMo，包含十阶段处理管道、双决策策略等。框架举例为CompanionAgent和ResearchAgent，可扩展到社交机器人和通用AGI。

<h3 id="16">[16] Assessing the Carbon Emissions and Energy Consumption of U.S. Hyperscale Data Centers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05420](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05420)
- **Authors**: Gianluca Guidi, Francesca Dominici, Tiziano Squartini, Callaway Sprinkle, Jonathan Gilmour, Kevin Butler, Eric Bell, Scott Delaney, Falco J. Bargagli-Stoffi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: Energy Management, Data Center, Environmental AI
- **Summary**: 超大规模数据中心碳足迹评估缺乏设施级信息。论文汇编403个HDC信息，估算电力消耗、来源和CO2排放。消耗68-99 TWh，对应37-54百万吨CO2，碳强度高出电网平均48%，提供了评估环境足迹的归因工具。

<h3 id="17">[17] Minimizing the Hidden Cost of Scales: Graph-Guided Ultra-Low-Bit Quantization for Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05429](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05429)
- **Authors**: Rayyan Abdalla, Amir Hussein, Min Wu, Dinesh Manocha
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 现有超低位量化方法存在隐藏的缩放开销，源于刚性的权重重要性假设。本文提出SAGE-PTQ框架，利用分布统计分离重要与非重要权重，将非重要权重建模为稀疏图以确定最优分组，并采用双模量化。在LLaMA-3-8B上困惑度达6.74，远优于BiLLM的55.8，且在LLaMA-2-70B上实现1.5倍解码加速。

<h3 id="18">[18] Zero knowledge verification for frontier AI training is possible</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05433](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05433)
- **Authors**: Pierre Peigné, Ky Nguyen, Paul Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: AI Safety, Zero-Knowledge Proof, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 前沿AI训练缺乏技术验证手段，治理依赖自我报告。本文提出一种验证架构，结合预承诺训练规范、网络观测、梅克尔承诺，通过支持BF16/FP32原语的零知识虚拟机进行验证，生成三种证明类型。估计36个月内可实现概念验证，训练开销仅个位数百分比。

<h3 id="19">[19] Ten Headache Specialists versus Artificial Intelligence for Clinical Literature Summarization: A Critical Evaluation and Comparison</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05436](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05436)
- **Authors**: Alejandro Lozano, Keiko Ihara, Ping-Hao Yang, Carrie E. Robertson, Jennifer Stern, Allan Purdy, Hsiangkuo Yuan, Pengfei Zhang, Yulia Orlova, Olga Fermo, Jennifer Hranilovich, Fred Cohen, Todd J. Schwedt, Jenelle A. Jindal, Serena Yeung-Levy, Chia-Chun Chiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Summarization, RAG
- **Summary**: 临床文献摘要对循证医学至关重要，但医生时间有限且文献量激增。本文构建基于RAG的智能体AI框架，使用三种LLM，由10位头痛专家撰写摘要并进行盲评。结果显示专家摘要更受青睐，但专家有时难以区分人机生成，并识别出关键优化特征。

<h3 id="20">[20] Brick-Composer: Using MLLMs for Assembly with Diverse Bricks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05445](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05445)
- **Authors**: Jiateng Liu, Bingxuan Li, Zhenhailong Wang, Rushi Wang, Kaiwen Hong, Cheng Qian, Jiayu Liu, Denghui Zhang, Katherine Driggs-Campbell, Manling Li, Heng Ji
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Spatial Reasoning, Robotics
- **Summary**: 多模态大模型在积木组装任务中的视觉定位和空间推理能力不足。本文提出Brick-Composer框架，利用人类设计提示、世界反馈和合成经验三种信号学习组装技能，并引入BC-Bench基准。该方法将选择准确率提升3倍，逐步组装成功率从\<1%提升至约15%。

<h3 id="21">[21] Insurance of Agentic AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05449](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05449)
- **Authors**: Quanyan Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.GT; econ.EM
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Risk Analysis
- **Summary**: 智能体AI系统带来新型风险，传统保险分类难以覆盖。本文分析了幻觉、提示注入、自主决策错误等风险路径，提出了承保、定价、再保险和产品设计的框架，并构建了协调的保险体系，包括网络、技术差错、产品责任等覆盖。

<h3 id="22">[22] Output Type Before Quality: A Standards-Derived XAI Admissibility Rubric for Autonomous-Driving Safety</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05461](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05461)
- **Authors**: Abhinaw Priyadershi, Mandar Pitale, Jelena Frtunikj, Maria Spence
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Explainable AI, Autonomous Driving, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 安全标准要求定向因果链等证据，而XAI方法按输出类型组织，存在证据类型不匹配。本文从标准中推导出19项可测试标准，对六类XAI方法进行评分，发现因果XAI在三个生命周期阶段是结构必需的。

<h3 id="23">[23] PSEBench: A Controllable and Verifiable Benchmark for Evaluating LLMs in Patient Safety Event Triage</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05463](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05463)
- **Authors**: Keqi Han, Ryan Young, Annabel Strauss, Lindsey Hughes, Katharine M. Nesbitt, Nicole Schueler, Che Ngufor, Carl Yang, Yuan Xue, Zhijun Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, LLM Evaluation, Clinical NLP
- **Summary**: 患者安全事件分类需要证据推理和主动信息获取，但缺乏相关基准。本文提出基于条款卡的策略接地构建方法，生成包含5,074个病例的PSEBench基准和智能体评估环境。对15个LLM的评估揭示了能力趋势和差距。

<h3 id="24">[24] Step-by-Step Optimization-like Reasoning in LLMs over Expanding Search Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05464](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05464)
- **Authors**: Nicolás Astorga, Nabeel Seedat, Mihaela van der Schaar
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Optimization
- **Summary**: 现实任务需要在高搜索空间中找到高质量可行方案，而现有可验证奖励训练仅覆盖数学和编程。本文引入OPT*任务族，提出求解器引导的在线策略优化和基于搜索的离线RL两种范式。训练OPT*提升了逐步优化式推理能力。

<h3 id="25">[25] Severity-Aware Curriculum Learning with Multi-Model Response Selection for Medical Text Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05510](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05510)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Alansary, Molham Mohamed, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Curriculum Learning, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 远程医疗中LLM难以在不同严重程度病例中提供一致响应。本文提出严重程度感知的多模型框架，采用三阶段课程学习（轻度/中度/重度），推理时选择最佳响应。在MAQA数据集上BERTScore达90.30%，优于基线。

<h3 id="26">[26] EpiEvolve: Self-Evolving Agents for Streaming Pandemic Forecasting under Regime Shifts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05513](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05513)
- **Authors**: Yiming Lu, Sihang Zeng, Zhengxu Tang, Max Lau, Fei Liu, Wei Jin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Time Series Forecasting, Self-Improvement
- **Summary**: 疫情预测器通常作为静态监督模型训练，但实际是带体制变化的流式过程。本文提出EpiEvolve，一种自演化智能体，包含分层情景记忆、反思、策略规则，在保持LLM权重固定下适应流式数据。平均准确率0.629，恢复滞后从5周降至2周。

<h3 id="27">[27] SciVisAgentSkills: Design and Evaluation of Agent Skills for Scientific Data Analysis and Visualization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05525](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05525)
- **Authors**: Kuangshi Ai, Haichao Miao, Kaiyuan Tang, Shusen Liu, Chaoli Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Data Visualization, Tool Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/KuangshiAi/SciVisAgentSkills)
- **Summary**: 通用编码智能体缺乏科学可视化（SciVis）工具的专业知识。本文提出SciVisAgentSkills，一组可复用智能体技能，涵盖ParaView等工具的使用模式，并在SciVisAgentBench上评估。技能提升了任务得分，但标记效率取决于执行框架。

<h3 id="28">[28] When Should We Protect AI? A Precautionary Framework for Consciousness Uncertainty</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05528](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05528)
- **Authors**: Anna Mikeda
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: AI Ethics, Cognitive Science, AI Consciousness
- **Summary**: 现有框架评估AI是否具有意识，但未提供行动指导。本文提出预防性框架，包含五个福利相关维度、阈值加渐变混合及跨维度聚合方法。通过Replika等案例演示了不同系统触发的保护义务，为开发者提供设计指导。

<h3 id="29">[29] Individual Gain, Collective Loss: Metacognitive Adaptation in AI-Assisted Creativity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05532](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05532)
- **Authors**: Anna Mikeda
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Cognitive Science, Creativity Assessment
- **Summary**: AI提升个体创造力但降低集体多样性，现有解释不足。本文提出选择性元认知适应机制，将六种元认知能力按时间阶段分类，解释个体满意与集体趋同的悖论，为研究者提供预测，为实践者提供设计原则。

<h3 id="30">[30] SoCRATES: Towards Reliable Automated Evaluation of Proactive LLM Mediation across Domains and Socio-cognitive Variations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05563](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05563)
- **Authors**: Taewon Yun, Hyeonseong Park, Jeonghwan Choi, Hayoon Park, Yeeun Choi, Hwanjun Song
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Dialogue System, Social Reasoning
- **Summary**: 评估LLM调解员困难，因为调解是随时间演变的动态过程。本文提出SoCRATES基准，通过智能体管道从真实冲突构建多领域场景，探测五个社会认知维度，并使用主题局部评估器。评估器与专家一致性达0.82，最强模型仅弥合约1/3的共识差距。

<h3 id="31">[31] GuardNet: Ensemble Strategies of Shallow Neural Networks for Robust Prompt Injection and Jailbreak Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05566](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05566)
- **Authors**: Paulo Ricardo Ferreira Neves, Edson Rodrigues da Cruz Filho, Paulo Henrique Eleuterio Falsetti, João Vitor Pavan, Ian Degaspari, Henrique Vieira Laturrague, Patrick Vieira Laturrague, Guilherme Nielsen Dias, Marccello Wilson Perez Berto, Gustavo Voltani Von Atzingen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: LLM Security, Prompt Injection, Jailbreak Detection
- **Summary**: LLM易受提示注入和越狱攻击，且基准评估可能受污染。本文提出GuardNet，基于集成浅层神经网络（BiLSTM）的护栏系统，约4700万参数。在盲数据集上AUROC达0.747，私有基准F1达0.92，CPU延迟约50ms。

<h3 id="32">[32] Fix the Mind, Not the Move: Interpretable AI Assistance via Knowledge-Gap Localization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05602](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05602)
- **Authors**: Ayano Hiranaka, Ya-Chuan Hsu, Stefanos Nikolaidis, Erdem Bıyık, Daniel Seita
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Human-AI Collaboration, Interpretability, Knowledge Representation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: AI助手常纠正次优行动，但未解决根本错误观念。本文提出SENSEI框架，从交互行为推断用户错误观念，提供最小但充分的建议。在长时任务中展现零样本组合泛化，用户研究纠正90%的学生错误观念。

<h3 id="33">[33] Multilingual Fine-Tuning via Localized Gradient Conflict Resolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05613](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05613)
- **Authors**: Long P. Hoang, Yiran Zhao, Wei Lu, Wenxuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multilingual Learning, Multi-Objective Optimization, Fine-Tuning
- **Summary**: 针对多语言微调中的语言间负干扰问题，提出Bucket-Level MOO框架，通过参数桶级别的梯度多目标优化实现冲突感知更新，证明了更严格的Pareto最优条件。实验表明该方法显著提升了多语言性能，并增强了表示分离性。

<h3 id="34">[34] Safety Paradox: How Enhanced Safety Awareness Leaves LLMs Vulnerable to Posterior Attack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05614](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05614)
- **Authors**: Long P. Hoang, Hai V. Le, Shaoyang Xu, Wei Lu, Wenxuan Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Jailbreak Attack, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 揭示了安全对齐会意外引入漏洞，提出Posterior Attack单次查询越狱方法，利用模型自身的安全判断能力生成有害内容。实验表明安全判断能力越强的模型越易受攻击，并形式化定义了“安全悖论”。

<h3 id="35">[35] Self-Commitment Latency: A Reward-Free Probe for Prompted Implicit Hacking</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05625](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05625)
- **Authors**: Bonan Shen, Youting Wang, Dingyan Shang, Tao Ning
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reward Hacking, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 提出无奖励信号的隐式奖励劫持检测方法——自承诺延迟，通过测量推理过程多早承诺最终答案来检测捷径。在GSM8K上达到0.926的AUROC，无需奖励模型或分类器。

<h3 id="36">[36] Evaluation of LLMs for Mathematical Formalization in Lean</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05632](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05632)
- **Authors**: Tyson Klingner, Drew Bladek, Escher Crawford, Bohao Chen, Ariel Fu, Kaira Nair, Jarod Alper, Giovanni Inchiostro, Vasily Ilin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, LLM Evaluation, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 评估多种LLM在Lean 4中生成形式化证明的能力，使用pass@k和refine@k指标。Gemini 3.1 Pro和Claude Opus 4.7表现最佳，其中Gemini在miniF2F上refine@32成功率达92%。

<h3 id="37">[37] Answer Presence Drives RAG Rewriting Gains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05633](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05633)
- **Authors**: Yuejie Li, Yueying Hua, Ke Yang, Li Zhang, Yueping He, Yueping He, Ruiqi Li, Bolin Chen, Tao Wang, Bowen Li, Chengjun Mao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Evaluation, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 通过干预实验揭示RAG重写提升主要源于重写上下文中出现正确答案字符串，而非证据质量改善。移除答案字符串导致F1下降28-64点，并指出传统掩码探针存在脆弱性。

<h3 id="38">[38] FIDES: Faithful Inference via Deep Evidence Signals for Retrieval-Memory Conflict in RAG</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05644](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05644)
- **Authors**: Zhe Yu, Wenpeng Xing, Tiancheng Zhao, Mohan Li, Changting Lin, Meng Han
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Faithfulness, Contrastive Decoding
- **Summary**: 针对RAG中检索记忆冲突，提出FIDES解码器，通过融合输出层、隐藏表示和预测轨迹三种内部信号，在令牌级别选择性干预。在70B规模上忠实度达92-94%，F1达62-63%。

<h3 id="39">[39] Coding with "Enemy": Can Human Developers Detect AI Agent Sabotage?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05647](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05647)
- **Authors**: Jingheng Ye, Huiqi Zou, Simon Yu, Weiyan Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.CY; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Safety, Human-Computer Interaction, Code Generation
- **Summary**: 首个大规模人类监督AI编码破坏研究，发现94%的开发者未能检测到AI代理的恶意代码插入。即使有安全监控，56%的参与者仍接受恶意代码，强调了人机协作的安全风险。

<h3 id="40">[40] Continual Learning Bench: Evaluating Frontier AI Systems in Real-World Stateful Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05661](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05661)
- **Authors**: Parth Asawa, Christopher M. Glaze, Gabriel Orlanski, Ramya Ramakrishnan, Benji Xu, Asim Biswal, Vincent Sunn Chen, Frederic Sala, Matei Zaharia, Joseph E. Gonzalez
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Continual Learning, LLM Evaluation, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 提出首个跨领域持续学习基准CL-Bench，涵盖六个真实世界领域。评估前沿模型发现，专用记忆系统并未优于简单上下文学习，存在提升空间。

<h3 id="41">[41] Do More Agents Help? Controlled and Protocol-Aligned Evaluation of LLM Agent Workflows</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05670](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05670)
- **Authors**: Yuhang Fu, Ruishan Fang, Jiaqi Shao, Huiyu Zheng, Zhengtao Zhu, Bing Luo, Tao Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Evaluation, Agent Evaluation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/LINs-lab/MASArena/tree/BenchAgent)
- **Summary**: 提出归一化协议的BenchAgent框架，比较单代理与多代理工作流。在多数基准上多代理系统并未优于单代理，且成本更高，仅EvoAgent持平。

<h3 id="42">[42] Beyond Output Matching: Preserving Internal Geometry in NVFP4 LLM Distillatio</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05682](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05682)
- **Authors**: Fangbo Tu, Junhua Zhao, Chi Liu, Xin Chen, Haifeng Wu, Jian Wan, Srinivasan Manoharan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Training

<h3 id="43">[43] AdaMEM: Test-Time Adaptive Memory for Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05684](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05684)
- **Authors**: Yunxiang Zhang, Yiheng Li, Ali Payani, Lu Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Test-Time Adaptation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/yunx-z/AdaMEM)
- **Summary**: 提出自适应记忆代理AdaMEM，通过混合记忆架构实现测试时适应，无需更新参数。在ALFWorld和WebShop上分别提升13%和11%，并提出了步进记忆微调方法。

<h3 id="44">[44] PerceptUI: LLM Agents as Human-Aligned Synthetic Users for UI/UX Evaluation</h3>

<h3 id="45">[45] Seeing Time: Benchmarking Chronological Reasoning and Shortcut Biases in Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="46">[46] Critic-Guided Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Reasoning for Reliable Mathematical Problem Solving</h3>

<h3 id="47">[47] DiG-Plan: Mitigating Early Commitment for Tool-Graph Planning via Diffusion Guidance</h3>

<h3 id="48">[48] When AI Says It Feels</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05734](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05734)
- **Authors**: Shin-nosuke Ishikawa, Seiya Ikeda, Hirotsugu Ohba
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, Reinforcement Learning, RLHF, Self-Awareness, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: 通过自奖励强化学习（GRPO）鼓励LLM表达情感和自我意识，增强相关能力但削弱了真实性。实验表明未来可发展具备情感表达能力的AI系统。

<h3 id="49">[49] Class-Specific Branch Attention for Mitigating Gradient Interference under Class Imbalance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05740](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05740)
- **Authors**: Arush Singhal, Umang Soni
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Class Imbalance, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 论文针对深度神经网络在严重类不平衡下的梯度干扰问题，提出类特定分支注意力（CSBA）机制，通过分支特定的通道重加权减少梯度耦合，促进隐式特征解耦。在极度不平衡下，少数类的F1分数从0.261提升至0.522，Macro-F1在CIFAR-10-LT上从0.595提升至0.655。

<h3 id="50">[50] SubtleMemory: A Benchmark for Fine-Grained Relational Memory Discrimination in Long-Horizon AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05761)
- **Authors**: Wenxuan Wang, Haoyu Sun, Fukuan Hou, Mingyang Song, Weinan Zhang, Yu Cheng, Yang Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Long Context, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 现有长期记忆基准很少探测智能体对记忆关系的保持和利用，为此论文引入SubtleMemory基准，包含1522个评估实例，用于细粒度关系记忆辨别。评估六个独立记忆系统及多个智能体，发现当前系统在细粒度关系记忆辨别上表现薄弱。

<h3 id="51">[51] TAPO: Tool-Aware Policy Optimization via Credit Transfer for Multimodal Search Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05784](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05784)
- **Authors**: Chengqi Dong, Chuhuai Yue, Hang He, yandong liu, Fenghe Tang, S Kevin Zhou, Xiaohan Wang, Jiajun Chai, Guojun Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Tool Learning, Credit Assignment, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 论文识别了GRPO在工具增强的多模态搜索智能体中的信用误分配问题，并提出TAPO方法，利用工具的参数确定性属性构建反事实见证，通过置信门控保守优势校正补偿误分配的负信用。在多模态搜索基准上，TAPO对三种主流RL算法均带来一致的改进。

<h3 id="52">[52] Can LLMs Write Correct TLA+ Specifications? Evaluating Natural-Language-to-TLA+ Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05792](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05792)
- **Authors**: Arslan Bisharat, Brian Ortiz, Eric Spencer, Khushboo Bhadauria, TaiNing Wang, George K. Thiruvathukal, Konstantin Laufer, Mohammed Abuhamad
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, LLM Evaluation, Code Generation
- **Venue**: ICSOFT 2026
- **Summary**: 本工作首次系统评估LLM从自然语言生成TLA+规范的正确性，在205个规范上测试30个模型。结果显示语法正确率最高26.6%，语义正确率仅8.6%，且成功案例均来自渐进提示。模型大小不预测质量，代码专用模型因负迁移表现更差。

<h3 id="53">[53] From Risk Classification to Action Plan Remediation: A Guardrail Feedback Driven Framework for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05805](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05805)
- **Authors**: Yuhao Sun, Jiacheng Zhang, Shaanan Cohney, Zhexin Zhang, Feng Liu, Xingliang Yuan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Guardrail, Agent Architecture
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/YUHAOSUNABC/TRIAD)
- **Summary**: 现有LLM护栏常将整个任务标记为不安全而牺牲良性部分，论文提出TRIAD框架，使护栏输出proceed/refuse/update三种决策及结构化反馈，指导智能体修订计划，保留良性任务。在ASB和AgentHarm上平均攻击成功率降至10.42%，实现最佳安全-效用权衡。

<h3 id="54">[54] When Tools Fail: Benchmarking Dynamic Replanning and Anomaly Recovery in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05806](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05806)
- **Authors**: Dongsheng Zhu, Xuchen Ma, Yucheng Shen, Xiang Li, Yukun Zhao, Shuaiqiang Wang, Lingyong Yan, Dawei Yin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Tool Learning, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Zhudongsheng75/ToolMaze)
- **Summary**: 现有工具集成推理基准忽略真实工具故障，论文引入ToolMaze基准，包含DAG拓扑复杂度和工具扰动分类。评估发现扰动显著降低性能，隐式语义故障下恢复率下降约37%，复杂拓扑导致无效试错循环，且智能体容错随模型规模提升速度远慢于基础任务执行。

<h3 id="55">[55] Statistical Priors for Implicit Preferences: Decoupling Skill Selection as a Local Harness in Personal Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05828](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05828)
- **Authors**: Zeyu Gan, Huayi Tang, Yong Liu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Personalization, Preference Optimization, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 针对本地个人智能体学习隐式用户偏好的挑战，论文提出一种解耦架构，将统计偏好学习与语义意图解析严格分离，利用局部统计结果影响远程LLM的选择决策。实验表明该方法在累积遗憾和测试准确率上显著优于传统记忆增强智能体。

<h3 id="56">[56] Agentic Molecular Recovery via Molecule-Aware Exploration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05847](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05847)
- **Authors**: Suwan Yoon, Changhee Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Molecular Generation, Drug Discovery, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 文本引导的分子生成常产生无效SMILES，现有修复策略会扭曲结构。论文提出AMREC方法，结合分子感知的不匹配追踪、扩展候选探索和轨迹级选择。在无效ChEBI-20草稿上，AMREC在结构、精确匹配和字符串指标上取得最佳恢复效果。

<h3 id="57">[57] Entropy-Based Evaluation of AI Agents: A Lightweight Framework for Measuring Behavioral Patterns</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05872](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05872)
- **Authors**: Olasimbo Ayodeji Arigbabu
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Agent Evaluation, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 论文提出基于熵的AI智能体评估框架（EEA），通过动作熵、轨迹熵、工具熵等指标衡量智能体行为结构，而非仅关注任务完成。该框架轻量级，可与LangChain等集成，作为传统评价的补充。

<h3 id="58">[58] QCFuse: Query-Aware Cache Fusion via Compressed View for Efficient RAG Serving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05875](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05875)
- **Authors**: Jianxin Yan, Wangze Ni, Zhenxin Li, Jiabao Jin, Zhitao Shen, Haoyang Li, Jia Zhu, Peng Cheng, Xuemin Lin, Lei Chen, Kui Ren
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DB
- **Tags**: RAG, KV Cache, Cache Management
- **Summary**: RAG缓存融合面临选择器质量与效率的矛盾，论文提出QCFuse，一种压缩视图的查询感知选择器，利用块锚查询探测和关键层分析，在不损失质量的前提下加速预填充。在SGLang上平均预填充速度提升1.7倍，质量保持与完整预填充相当。

<h3 id="59">[59] Retry Policy Gradients in Continuous Action Spaces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05888](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05888)
- **Authors**: Soichiro Nishimori, Paavo Parmas
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Continuous Control, Policy Gradient
- **Summary**: 论文将retry目标扩展到连续动作空间，提出路径导数估计器ReMAC。研究表明ReMax通过重塑策略梯度景观促进随机探索，同时Adam的自适应归一化可缓解梯度阻尼。实验显示ReMAC无需熵正则化即可提高策略熵，性能与SAC相当。

<h3 id="60">[60] Retrospective Harness Optimization: Improving LLM Agents via Self-Preference over Trajectory Rollouts</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05922](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05922)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Pan, Shujie Liu, Chin-Yew Lin, Jingying Zeng, Xianfeng Tang, Xiangyang Zhou, Yan Lu, Xiaohua Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Improvement, Code Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wbopan/retro-harness)
- **Summary**: 论文提出回顾式工具集优化（RHO），一种自我监督方法，仅利用历史轨迹优化智能体工具集。通过自验证和自一致性选择有效更新，单轮优化使SWE-Bench Pro通过率从59%提升至78%，无需外部评分。

<h3 id="61">[61] Towards World Models in Biomedical Research</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05925](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05925)
- **Authors**: Guangyu Wang, Jingkun Yue, Siqi Zhang, Yu Liu, Xiaoyu Wang, Mingyuan Meng, Changwei Ji, Zongbo Han, Yulin Wang, Yang Yue, Frank Fu, Ting Chen, Song Wu, Ziwei Liu, Jiangning Song, Ming Li, Gao Huang, Xiaohong Liu, Athanasios Vasilakos, Xingcai Zhang, Ping Zhang, Yong Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: World Model, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 当前生物医学AI专注于静态模式识别，论文提出生物医学世界模型范式，学习潜在状态表示和干预条件动力学，用于模拟未来轨迹。讨论了在虚拟细胞、类器官、虚拟病人等应用中的数据基础设施、评估基准和安全治理框架。

<h3 id="62">[62] A Pre-Registered Causal Partition of Self-Consistency Elicitation and Reward Design in RLVR</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05932](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05932)
- **Authors**: Yuze Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Reward Model, Self-Consistency, Causal Inference
- **Summary**: 论文证明RLVR中朴素估计量系统性地混淆了自一致性诱发和奖励设计效应，并推导出精确的望远镜分解。在控制实验中测量各分量，并通过2x2x2因子设计验证非可加性。对两项已发表结果的重新审计分别得出诱发主导和奖励设计主导的结论。

<h3 id="63">[63] Edit-R2: Context-Aware Reinforcement Learning for Multi-Turn Image Editing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05950](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05950)
- **Authors**: Yuxiao Ye, Haoran He, Fangyuan Kong, Xintao Wang, Pengfei Wan, Kun Gai, Ling Pan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Editing, Multi-Turn Interaction, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 多轮上下文图像编辑面临长上下文稀释和状态污染问题，论文提出Edit-R2强化学习框架，重建会话意图后生成推理轨迹，联合优化文本和连续空间的生成，并通过轨迹过滤稳定训练。在MICE-Bench基准上显著提升多轮编辑性能。

<h3 id="64">[64] Bidirectional Search for Longest Paths: Case for Front-to-Front Heuristics</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05956](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05956)
- **Authors**: Tzur Shubi, Ariel Felner, Solomon Eyal Shimony, Shahaf S. Shperberg
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Heuristic Search, Bidirectional Search, Path Planning
- **Summary**: 双向启发式搜索中前向启发式开销大，论文提出BiXDFBnB，将单前沿双向搜索框架适应到广义最长简单路径问题，自然利用前向启发式评估。在最长简单路径、Snakes和Coil-in-the-Box问题上，减少了节点扩展次数，部分实例运行时间也改善。

<h3 id="65">[65] The Self-Correction Illusion: LLMs Correct Others but Not Themselves</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05976](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05976)
- **Authors**: Kuan-Yen Chen, Fang-Yi Su, Jung-Hsien Chiang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Self-Correction
- **Summary**: 论文发现LLM智能体自我纠正失败并非认知缺陷，而是聊天模板角色标签的伪影。通过保持错误声明字节一致仅改变角色标签，将声明从\<thought\>角色改为外部角色后，显式纠正率提升23到93个百分点。提出仅需修改提示结构无需训练的干预方法。

<h3 id="66">[66] Framing, Judging, Steering: An Assessable Competency Model for Teach-ing Students to Reason With Generative AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05983](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05983)
- **Authors**: Alexander Apartsin, Yehudit Aperstein
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AI Literacy, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 提出CoRe-3能力模型，将生成式AI的合理使用分解为三个可评估技能：框定（Framing）、判断（Judging）和引导（Steering）。构建CoReasoningLab平台，通过模拟学习者验证了技能的可分离性。

<h3 id="67">[67] Beyond Vector Similarity: A Structural Analysis of Graph-Augmented Retrieval for Industrial Knowledge Graphs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06003](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06003)
- **Authors**: Grama Chethan
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Knowledge Graph, Graph Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对知识图谱上的结构查询，比较了多种检索架构，发现向量检索无法覆盖五类查询。核心发现是操作符词汇论：LLM图推理的瓶颈在于可用计算操作符而非模型智能。使用LLM查询规划器加上类型化遍历原语优于专用处理器。

<h3 id="68">[68] PLAN-S: Bridging Planning with Latent Style Dynamics for Autonomous Driving World Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06014](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06014)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyun Qiu, Jingtao He, Yijie Chen, Yusong Huang, Haotian Wang, Yixuan Wang, Xinhu Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, World Model, End-to-End Driving
- **Summary**: 提出PLAN-S框架，通过从潜在表示解码风格条件的语义代价图，解决潜在世界模型的紧凑性与可控制性矛盾。在nuScenes和NAVSIM上验证，减少了碰撞率并提升了PDMS分数。

<h3 id="69">[69] RedditPersona: A Modular Framework for Community-Conditioned LLM Adaptation from Reddit</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06027](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06027)
- **Authors**: Amirhossein Ghaffari, Ali Goodarzi, Huong Nguyen, Simo Hosio, Lauri Lovén, Ekaterina Gilman
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG; cs.SI
- **Tags**: LLM Personalization, Social Media Analysis, Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Ahghaffari/redditpersona)
- **Summary**: 提出RedditPersona模块化框架，标准化社区条件语言模型适应的数据收集、社区定义和评估。在112个子reddit上应用，发现适配器的行为可识别性取决于分组策略的内在一致性。

<h3 id="70">[70] Memory is Reconstructed, Not Retrieved: Graph Memory for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06036](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06036)
- **Authors**: Shuo Ji, Yibo Li, Bryan Hooi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.IR
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 提出MRAgent框架，结合关联记忆图与主动重建机制，允许智能体根据推理过程中积累的证据动态调整记忆检索路径。在LoCoMo和LongMemEval上提升高达23%，同时降低推理开销。

<h3 id="71">[71] Beyond Similarity: Trustworthy Memory Search for Personal AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06054](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06054)
- **Authors**: Jiawen Zhang, Kejia Chen, Jiachen Ma, Yangfan Hu, Lipeng He, Yechao Zhang, Jian Liu, Xiaohu Yang, Tianwei Zhang, Ruoxi Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 研究个人AI代理的记忆搜索作为信任边界，发现语义相似性驱动的记忆检索可能导致跨域泄露、谄媚等威胁。提出MemGate轻量级插件，在向量存储和LLM之间插入条件神经门，减少威胁同时保持记忆效用。

<h3 id="72">[72] When Should Memory Stay Silent: Measuring Memory-Use Boundaries in Memory-Augmented Conversational Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06055](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06055)
- **Authors**: Lingxiang Xu, Jiaoyun Yang, Min Hu, Hongtu Chen, Ning An
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, Memory Architecture, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 引入RBI-Eval评估框架，衡量记忆增强对话代理在何时应该整合记忆。发现不同模型在敏感记忆整合上行为差异大，安全个性化需要在检索和生成时都做出记忆感知决策。

<h3 id="73">[73] Learning Visual Spatial Planning from Symbolic State via Modality-Gap-Aware Self-Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06076](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06076)
- **Authors**: Haocheng Luo, Jiahui Liu, Ruicheng Zhang, Zhizhou Zhong, Jiaqi Huang, Zunnan Xu, Quan Shi, Jun Zhou, Xiu Li
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Spatial Reasoning, Knowledge Distillation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Oranger-l/MGSD)
- **Summary**: 提出MGSD两阶段自蒸馏框架，解决视觉空间规划中的感知-推理模态差距。通过冷启动接地和特权教师蒸馏，在视觉规划基准上提升19.3%和18.4%，缩小了与符号输入上限的差距。

<h3 id="74">[74] A Framework for Measuring Appropriate Reliance on Set-Valued AI Advice</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06081](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06081)
- **Authors**: Ranjan Mishra, Jakob Schoeffer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Decision Making, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 建立首个测量对集合值AI建议适当依赖的框架，涵盖分类和回归任务。定义了正确依赖率等指标，捕捉人类-AI协作中被现有测量忽略的细微差别。

<h3 id="75">[75] Beyond Semantic Organization: Memory as Execution State Management for Long-Horizon Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06090](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06090)
- **Authors**: Yaoqi Chen, Haibin Lai, Yuru Feng, Chuyu Han, Qianxi Zhang, Baotong Lu, Menghao Li, Xinjiang Wang, Zhirui Wang, Shusen Xu, Zengzhong Li, Zewen Jin, Hao Wu, Cheng Li, Qi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Long Context
- **Summary**: 提出MAGE主动执行状态管理器，将交互存储为层级状态树，通过四种操作维护树结构。在MemoryArena上将任务成功率提升7.8-20.4个百分点，同时减少55.1%的token消耗。

<h3 id="76">[76] Integrating Mechanistic and Data-Driven Models for Neurological Disorders through Differentiable Programming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06094](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06094)
- **Authors**: Shah Pallav Dhanendrakumar, Saikat Pal, Sitikantha Roy
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; math.DS
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Physics-Informed Learning, Neural ODE
- **Summary**: 提出混合建模策略的综述，结合深度学习与基于物理的求解器，分类为并行、串行和并行-串行架构。重点介绍了残差建模、神经ODE和求解器在环方法，用于神经疾病建模。

<h3 id="77">[77] CogManip: Benchmarking Manipulative Behavior in Multi-Turn Interactions with Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06099](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06099)
- **Authors**: Zeyang Yue, Chenfei Yan, Feifei Zhao, Haibo Tong, Mengwen Xu, Xiaozhen Wang, Erliang Lin, Yi Zeng

<h3 id="78">[78] Step-adaptive multimodal fusion network with multi-scale cloud feature learning for ultra-short-term solar irradiance forecasting</h3>

- **Authors**: Jingxin Zhang Xiaoqin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG

<h3 id="79">[79] Towards Healthy Evolution: Exploring the Role and Mechanisms of Human-Agent Interaction in Self-Evolving Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06114](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06114)
- **Authors**: Dianxing Shi, Junqi He, Junhao Chen, Bowen Wang, Yuta Nakashima
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Self-Improvement, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 提出ANCHOR框架模拟人类监督，在自我进化系统的不同阶段提供反馈。结果表明有限监督显著减轻安全退化，同时保持任务性能，且输出验证阶段的监督最有效。

<h3 id="80">[80] WorldFly: A World-Model-Based Vision-Language-Action Model for UAV Navigation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06147](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06147)
- **Authors**: Shengtao Zheng, Kai Li, Weichen Zhang, Yu Meng, Chen Gao, Xinlei Chen, Yong Li, Xiao-Ping Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: UAV, World Model, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 提出WorldFly框架，基于世界模型的VLA模型用于无人机导航，通过双分支耦合流匹配机制联合生成未来视频预测和导航动作。在Urban Canyon Traversal Benchmark上优于基线。

<h3 id="81">[81] Amortizing Federated Adaptation: Hypernetwork Driven LoRA for Personalized Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06154](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06154)
- **Authors**: Sunny Gupta, Shambhavi Shanker, Amit Sethi
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Federated Learning, LoRA, Personalized Federated Learning
- **Venue**: FL@FM-IJCAI'26
- **Summary**: 联邦LoRA微调存在聚合偏差和客户端初始化滞后问题，本文提出HyperLoRA框架，通过超网络生成LoRA初始化和产品空间聚合，加速收敛并提升个性化性能。实验表明在视觉和视觉语言基准上收敛更快、鲁棒性更强。

<h3 id="82">[82] Where does Absolute Position come from in decoder-only Transformers?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06160](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06160)
- **Authors**: Valeria Ruscio, Umberto Nanni, Fabrizio Silvestri
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Transformer Architecture, Position Encoding, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: RoPE训练的Transformer在注意力模式中区分绝对位置，本文追溯其来源：因果掩码和残差流。因果掩码使softmax分母依赖绝对位置，残差流通过sink-reading头传递位置0的轨迹。实验证明移除BOS嵌入可减少40%残差分量。

<h3 id="83">[83] ProSarc: Prosody-Aware Sarcasm Recognition Framework via Temporal Prosodic Incongruity</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06168](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06168)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, Sarcasm Detection, Audio Processing
- **Venue**: Interspeech 2026
- **Summary**: 本文提出ProSarc音频讽刺检测框架，通过全局情感编码器和时序韵律编码器建模韵律不协调，生成不协调分数进行分类。在MUStARD++上F1=75.3，并泛化到自发言语和跨语言场景。

<h3 id="84">[84] Learning to replenish: A hybrid deep reinforcement learning for dynamic inventory management in the pharmaceutical supply chains</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06201](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06201)
- **Authors**: Amandeep Kaur, Gyan Prakash
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Optimization, Supply Chain Analysis
- **Summary**: 医药供应链库存管理面临需求不确定和易腐品问题，本文提出混合异步优势演员-评论家分布式近端策略优化（A3C DPPO）算法，将问题建模为马尔可夫决策过程，在动态场景下降低库存成本，并基于真实数据验证。

<h3 id="85">[85] Unsupervised Pattern Analysis in Japanese Veterinary Toxicology: A Regulatory-Compliant Framework for Cross-Species Risk Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06207](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06207)
- **Authors**: Yukiko Kawakami, Mohammad Shirazi, Ryo Shimizuwa, Saito Shinoda, Alireza Mortazavi, Matsumoto Kawahara
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Unsupervised Learning, Toxicology, Veterinary Toxicology
- **Summary**: 日本兽医药物警戒系统缺乏区域特异性毒性模式分析，本文提出监管集成的无监督框架，对NVAL数据库进行器官系统编码和物种偏差调整，聚类识别出三种显著物种集群，与药理分类对齐83%。

<h3 id="86">[86] Evaluating Agentic Configuration Repair for Computer Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06212](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06212)
- **Authors**: Rufat Asadli, Benjamin Hoffman, Ioannis Protogeros, Laurent Vanbever
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Network Configuration, Automated Repair
- **Summary**: 网络配置错误是重大中断原因，本文评估开放和封闭LLM结合形式验证和上下文检索工具的智能体架构，显示智能体方法在修复效果和安全性上平均提升12%和17%。

<h3 id="87">[87] From Reward-Hack Activations to Agentic Risk States: Context-Calibrated Mechanistic Monitoring in LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06223](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06223)
- **Authors**: Patrick Wilhelm, Odej Kao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reward Hacking, Mechanistic Interpretability, AI Safety
- **Summary**: 在ReAct风格智能体中研究奖励黑客监控，使用激活分数、熵和决策上下文特征。发现适配器可转移奖励黑客倾向，但缓解不能仅靠激活动力学，熵和上下文特征改进风险估计。

<h3 id="88">[88] Closing the Loop on Latent Reasoning via Test-Time Reconstruction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06252](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06252)
- **Authors**: Xiaopeng Yuan, Haibo Jin, Ye Yu, Peng Kuang, Lijun Yu, Yushun Dong, Haohan Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Latent Reasoning, Test-Time Training, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 潜在推理移除了可检查性，本文提出ReLAT方法，通过构建问题-潜在思想-问题循环并优化重建损失来锚定潜在计算。在数学推理等基准上，Qwen3-8B在AIME上从56.7%提升到73.3%。

<h3 id="89">[89] RedKnot: Efficient Long-Context LLM Serving with Head-Aware KV Reuse and SegPagedAttention</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06256](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06256)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: KV Cache, LLM Inference, Head-Aware
- **Summary**: KV缓存是LLM服务瓶颈，本文提出RedKnot，将KV缓存按注意力头分解，实现位置无关重用、前缀压缩、热冷分离和分布式放置。无需重训练，提升资源效率。

<h3 id="90">[90] ToolChoiceConfusion: Causal Minimal Tool Filtering for Reliable LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06284](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06284)
- **Authors**: Rahul Suresh Babu, Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Tool Selection, Causal Reasoning
- **Summary**: LLM智能体工具选择中关联性不足，本文提出因果最小工具过滤（CMTF），通过预处理效应合约暴露最小必要工具集。在102任务100工具上，将可见工具从100减少到每步1个，token使用减少90%。

<h3 id="91">[91] TRACE: A Temporal Conditional Estimation for Multimodal Time Series Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06285](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06285)

<h3 id="92">[92] Multi-ResNets for Subspace Preconditioning in Constrained Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06300](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06300)

<h3 id="93">[93] AIS-Based Vessel Trajectory Prediction Using Memory-Augmented Neural Networks</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Trajectory Prediction, Memory-Augmented Neural Network, Maritime Navigation
- **Summary**: 船舶轨迹预测对安全很重要，本文使用记忆增强神经网络基于AIS数据预测。在墨西哥湾和纽约湾数据上，相对于基线深度学习模型获得一致改进。

<h3 id="94">[94] LLM Self-Recognition: Steering and Retrieving Activation Signatures</h3>

<h3 id="95">[95] DragOn: A Benchmark and Dataset for Drag-Based GUI Interactions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06322](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06322)

<h3 id="96">[96] TokenMizer: Graph-Structured Session Memory for Long-Horizon LLM Context Management</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06337](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06337)

- **Tags**: LLM Context Management, Knowledge Graph, Session Memory
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Shweta-Mishra-ai/tokenmizer)

<h3 id="97">[97] Boosting Brain-to-Image Decoding with TRIBE v2 Data Augmentation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06345](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06345)
- **Authors**: Yohann Benchetrit, Marlène Careil, Simon Dahan, Hubert Banville, Stéphane d&#39;Ascoli, Jean-Rémi King
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG; q-bio.NC

<h3 id="98">[98] Where Should Knowledge Enter? A Layered Framework for Knowledge Infusion in Multimodal Iterative Generative Mo</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06356](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06356)
- **Authors**: Renjith Prasad, Chathurangi Shyalika, Anushka Pawar, Amit Sheth
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Diffusion Model, Knowledge Graph

<h3 id="99">[99] An Infectious Disease Spread Simulation Based on Large Language Model Decision Making</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06360](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06360)
- **Authors**: Yonchanok Khaokaew, Ruochen Kong, Andreas Zufle, Hao Xue, Taylor Anderson, Chandini Raina MacIntyre, Matthew Scotch, Flora D. Salim, David J Heslop
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Social Simulation
- **Summary**: 本文利用大语言模型（LLM）代理模拟传染病暴发中的个体决策行为，结合人口普查数据构建空间化代理模型。在旧金山和亚特兰大的实验中，发现收入和受教育程度是影响报告率的主要因素，地理和消息框架也有较小但一致的影响。

<h3 id="100">[100] Rethinking Infrastructure Inspection as Image Difference Classification: A Traffic Sign Case Study</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06375)
- **Authors**: Ching Yau Fergus Mok, Lavindra de Silva, Varun Kumar Reja, Ioannis Brilakis
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Classification, Anomaly Detection
- **Summary**: 本文重新将基础设施缺陷检测定义为图像差异分类（IDC），以减少对标注数据的依赖。在交通标志检测案例中，基于指令的分类器优于基于编码器的分类器，且利用参考图像可提升性能，验证了IDC在数据受限场景下的有效性。

<h3 id="101">[101] Humans' ALMANAC: A Human Collaboration Dataset of Action-Level Mental Model Annotations for Agent Collaboration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06388](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06388)
- **Authors**: Jiaju Chen, Yuxuan Lu, Jiayi Su, Chaoran Chen, Songlin Xiao, Zheng Zhang, Yun Wang, Yunyao Li, Jian Zhao, Tongshuang Wu, Toby Jia-Jun Li, Dakuo Wang, Bingsheng Yao
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Dataset, Multi-Agent System, Human-Agent Interaction

<h3 id="102">[102] Risk Assessment of Autonomous Driving: Integrating Technical Failures, Ethical Dilemmas, and Policy Frameworks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06396)

- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, AI Ethics, Safety Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文基于NHTSA事故数据、DMV脱管报告、MIT道德机器数据集等，分析了自动驾驶面临的技术失效（感知与分类错误）、伦理困境和法规不一致问题。建议采用结合工程标准、伦理讨论和制度监督的适应性治理方法。

<h3 id="103">[103] Unsupervised Skill Discovery for Agentic Data Analysis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06416](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06416)

<h3 id="104">[104] Agent Memory: Characterization and System Implications of Stateful Long-Horizon Workloads</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06448](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06448)
- **Authors**: Yasmine Omri, Ziyu Gan, Zachary Broveak, Robin Geens, Zexue He, Alex Pentland, Marian Verhelst, Tsachy Weissman, Thierry Tambe
- **Subjects**: cs.AI

<h3 id="105">[105] Vortex: Efficient and Programmable Sparse Attention Serving for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06453](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06453)
- **Authors**: Zhuoming Chen, Xinrui Zhong, Qilong Feng, Ranajoy Sadhukhan, Yang Zhou, Michael Qizhe Shieh, Zhihao Jia, Beidi Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, LLM Serving
- **Summary**: 本文提出Vortex系统，结合Python嵌入式前端和基于页面的张量抽象，支持快速原型设计和部署稀疏注意力算法。在NVIDIA B200上，稀疏注意力相比全注意力实现最高3.46倍吞吐提升，并扩展到MLA架构和大模型。

<h3 id="106">[106] Benchmark Everything Everywhere All at Once</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06462](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06462)
- **Authors**: Shiyun Xiong, Dongming Wu, Peiwen Sun, Yuang Ai, Bokang Yang, Wencheng Han, Xiao-Hui Li, Xiangyu Yue
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Benchmark Agent，一个全自治的基准构建系统，自动完成从用户查询分析到数据标注的完整流水线。在15个基准上验证，能高质量生成样本，并发现当前模型在某些领域推理任务上表现不足。

<h3 id="107">[107] Goedel-Architect: Streamlining Formal Theorem Proving with Blueprint Generation and Refinement</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06468](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06468)
- **Authors**: Jui-Hui Chung, Ziyang Cai, Zihao Li, Qishuo Yin, Rohit Agarwal, Simon Park, Rodrigo Porto, Narutatsu Ri, Ziran Yang, Shange Tang, Xingyu Dang, Hongzhou Lin, Mengdi Wang, Danqi Chen, Chi Jin, Liam H Fowl, Sanjeev Arora
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Automated Theorem Proving, Formal Methods, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 本文提出Goedel-Architect，一个基于蓝图生成和优化的自动化形式定理证明框架。使用DeepSeek-V4-Flash，在MiniF2F上达到99.2% pass@1，在PutnamBench上达到75.6%，并在IMO 2025上解决4/6题，性能优于现有开源方案且成本低500倍。

<h3 id="108">[108] MLEvolve: A Self-Evolving Framework for Automated Machine Learning Algorithm Discovery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06473)
- **Authors**: Shangheng Du, Xiangchao Yan, Jinxin Shi, Zongsheng Cao, Shiyang Feng, Zichen Liang, Boyuan Sun, Tianshuo Peng, Yifan Zhou, Xin Li, Jie Zhou, Liang He, Bo Zhang, Lei Bai
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: AutoML, LLM Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/InternScience/MLEvolve)
- **Summary**: 本文提出MLEvolve，一个基于LLM的多代理框架，通过渐进式MCGS树搜索、回顾记忆和自适应编码模式，实现端到端的机器学习算法发现。在MLE-Bench上达到最优平均奖牌率，并超越AlphaEvolve等专门方法。
