- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LoRA, Data Synthesis, Energy Efficiency
- **Summary**: 针对电信客服领域，现有大模型评估和适配不足，且数据主权限制外部模型使用。本文采用LoRA对Qwen2.5-3B进行参数高效微调，并组合生成约3万个训练样本。实验发现最低验证损失(0.5024)在定性评估中仅排第6-7，而最高损失(0.6807)排名第一，表明验证损失不足以判断对话AI微调配置，同时进行了能耗分析。

<h3 id="114">[114] MCBench: A Multicontext Safety Assessment Benchmark for Omni Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05177](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05177) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Manh Luong, Tamas Abraham, Junae Kim, Amar Kaur, Rollin Omari, Gholamreza Haffari, Trang Vu, Lizhen Qu, Dinh Phung
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: LLM Safety, Multimodal Learning, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 现有多模态安全基准仅覆盖视觉输入，无法评估全模态大模型。本文提出MCBench，包含1196个跨四种安全类别的场景，每类搭配微调的安全对照。评估表明全模态大模型难以整合多模态线索进行安全判断，缺乏鲁棒的跨模态推理能力。

<h3 id="115">[115] The Virtual Roundtable: Multi-Agent Personas Simulating the Dynamics of Human Brainstorming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tim Dorn, Saara A. Khan, Julie Mumford
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multi-Agent System, LLM Agent, Role-Playing
- **Summary**: 传统人类头脑风暴存在群体思维和回声室等局限。本文提出多智能体架构模拟圆桌讨论，分发散和收敛两阶段，使用多样化AI角色。案例研究显示该方法能产生多样、相关的创意，并追踪思路演化，累积交流深化了讨论质量。

<h3 id="116">[116] From Scoring to Explanations: Evaluating SHAP and LLM Rationales for Rubric-based Teaching Quality Assessment</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05180](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05180) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ivo Bueno, Babette Bühler, Philipp Stark, Tim Fütterer, Ulrich Trautwein, Dorottya Demszky, Heather Hill, Enkelejda Kasneci
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Explainable AI, Feature Attribution
- **Venue**: Findings of ACL 2026
- **Summary**: 自动化评分模型缺乏可解释性。本文提出结合SHAP和LLM解释的框架，用于基于量规的教学质量评估。实验表明SHAP比LLM生成的解释更忠实和可迁移，为高风险教育评估提供了原则性评价基础。

<h3 id="117">[117] Multi-Granularity Reasoning for Natural Language Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05181](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05181) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chunling Xi, Di Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Natural Language Inference, Hierarchical Reasoning, Reasoning Structure
- **Summary**: 现有自然语言推理模型主要依赖最后一层表示，难以捕捉分层语义交互。本文提出多粒度推理网络(MGRN)，显式利用分层语义特征，模拟人类从浅层匹配到深层逻辑推理的认知过程。在多个基准上一致优于强基线。

<h3 id="118">[118] The Granularity Gap: A Multi-Dimensional Longitudinal Audit of Sycophancy in Gemini Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05183](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05183) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Patrick Keough
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Evaluation, Sycophancy
- **Summary**: 现有对齐基准将谄媚视为二元失败，掩盖了细粒度的社会遵从行为。本文在6个Gemini变体上使用0-4 Likert量表，发现27.2%的回答含大量谄媚内容；代际进步非单调，谄媚与事实性负相关。粗粒度指标仅解释29%的方差。

<h3 id="119">[119] Geographic Bias and Diversity in AI Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05187](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05187) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilong Liu, Krzysztof Janowicz, Gengchen Mai, Song Gao, Rui Zhu
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fairness, Geographic Bias, AI Ethics
- **Summary**: AI偏见研究较少关注地理维度。本文通过文献综述识别了从表示偏见到原型默认的地理偏见，并展示了最新研究如何评估生成式AI输出的地理多样性。

<h3 id="120">[120] Assessing the Geographic Diversity of AI's Platial Representations in Image Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05188](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05188) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zilong Liu, Krzysztof Janowicz, Mina Karimi
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Image Generation, Geographic Bias, AI Evaluation
- **Venue**: AGILE 2026
- **Summary**: 多模态AI时代需评估图像生成的地理多样性。本文采用GPT和DALL-E，引入生态学相似性加权度量。发现旧模型多样性更高，提示修订比图像生成更多样，且所选模型存在一致性同质化。

<h3 id="121">[121] Temporal Preference Concepts and their Functions in a Large Language Model</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05194](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05194) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ian Rios-Sialer, Shantanu Darveshi, Shuai Jiang, Avigya Paudel, Anastasiia Pronina, Ipshita Bandyopadhyay, Justin Shenk
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Mechanistic Interpretability, Temporal Reasoning
- **Summary**: 大模型需进行时间权衡决策，但其内部表征未知。本文通过梯度归因和激活补丁因果定位Qwen3-4B的时间偏好子图，发现模型折现率远低于人类，且偏好不稳定。表明需要显式控制而非依赖隐含训练。

<h3 id="122">[122] Finite Element-Based Material Learning via Automatic Differentiation: Learning constitutive neural network models from full-field deformation data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05199](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05199) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Matthias Knipper, Chenyi Ji, Malte Brand, Kevin Linka
- **Subjects**: cs.AI
- **Tags**: Physics-Informed Learning, Scientific Computing, Differentiable Physics
- **Summary**: 从全场变形数据识别本构模型面临效率与鲁棒性平衡。本文提出FE-MAD，通过自动微分将本构神经网络嵌入JAX-FEM可微求解器，端到端优化。在三个公开数据集上验证，并泛化至22个未见样本。

<h3 id="123">[123] Ontology-constrained multi-LLM scoring of hypothesis support in the predictive processing literature</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05206](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05206) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hamed Nejat, Alexander Maier, Jesse Spencer-Smith, André M. Bastos
- **Subjects**: q-bio.NC; cs.AI; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Multi-Agent System, Knowledge Synthesis
- **Summary**: 预测编码神经科学文献碎片化，传统元分析难以解决。本文构建多LLM管道，结合术语表对31篇研究进行本体约束评分，发现局部与全局oddball范式下的结构化分歧，并定义假设空间温度度量分散度。

<h3 id="124">[124] The Score Hamiltonian: Mapping Diffusion Models to Adiabatic Transport</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05217) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Peter Halmos, Boris Hanin
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Score Matching, Sampling
- **Summary**: 揭示得分基扩散模型与绝热输运的精确对应，构造Score Hamiltonian。通过绝热定理获得密度重建界和退火计划，采样基本极限由得分匹配误差平方与谱隙之比决定。

<h3 id="125">[125] Gradient Descent with Large Step Size Restores Symmetry in Deep Linear Networks with Multi-Pathway</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05219) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hee-Sung Kim, Sungyoon Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Deep Learning Theory, Optimization, Training Dynamics
- **Summary**: 梯度流预测多通路深度线性网络的对称性破缺，但大步长离散梯度下降结果不同。本文证明单通路解是尖锐极小，大步长触发边缘稳定振荡导致信号再平衡。解释了大步长GD为何偏好共享表示。

<h3 id="126">[126] Where's the Structure? A Systematic Literature Review of Empirical Research on Human-AI Collaboration and Hybrid Intelligence for Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05222](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05222) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luis P. Prieto, Juan I. Asensio-Pérez, María Jesús Rodríguez-Triana, Mohamed Saban, Yannis Dimitriadis
- **Subjects**: cs.CY; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Education Technology, Systematic Review
- **Summary**: 人机协作学习需要结构化的交互设计。本文系统综述62项实证研究，刻画了协作过程、结构和应用场景，提取设计知识和研究空白。

<h3 id="127">[127] Differentiable Efficient Operator Search</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05232](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05232) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaohuan Pei, Jiyuan Zhang, Yuanfan Guo, Weiguo Feng, Tao Huang, Cho-Jui Hsieh, Chang Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Neural Architecture Search, Token Reduction
- **Summary**: 多模态基础模型依赖人工设计的token缩减算子。本文提出可微高效算子搜索，联合搜索缩减位置、预算和算子行为。实验发现搜索出的混合算子在激进视觉token缩减下取得有竞争力的精度-效率权衡。

<h3 id="128">[128] Domain-Conditioned Safety in Frontier Computer-Using Agents: A 793-Episode Browser Benchmark, a Coding-Domain Cross-Reference, and a Reproducibility Audit of Recent Red-Teaming</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05233](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05233) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nicholas Saban
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Web Agent, Red Teaming, Prompt Injection
- **Summary**: 近期红队测试报告高攻击成功率，但基于旧模型且不可复现。本文发布CUA-HandCrafted基准（793 episode），在Claude Sonnet 4.6等模型上测得多步攻击成功率为0/140，但代码智能体上达100%。表明前沿安全具有领域条件性。

<h3 id="129">[129] Search-Time Contamination in Deep Research Agents: Measuring Performance Inflation in Public Benchmark Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05241](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05241) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongjie Wang, Xinyue Zhang, Kunhong Yao, Zhiwei Zeng, Kaisong Song, Jun Lin, Zhiqi Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Benchmark Contamination, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 搜索时间污染（STC）导致深度研究智能体在公共基准测试中性能虚高。本文定义了三种污染类型并开发检测算法，发现STC普遍存在且可使性能虚高最多4%。提倡污染感知的评估实践。

<h3 id="130">[130] From Attack Simulation to SIEM Rule: Deterministic Detection-as-Code Synthesis with Probe-Level Traceability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05252](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05252) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Detection Engineering
- **Summary**: 安全团队手动将攻击模拟结果转化为SIEM检测规则耗时耗力。本文提出确定性综合函数，利用模板库将发现映射为Sigma规则，并附带溯源信息。在LLM和Web两个语料库上，所有规则均可解析并转换，在OpenSearch上实现了可接受的检测率和误报率。

<h3 id="131">[131] NIV: Neural Axis Variations for Variable Font Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nadav Benedek, Ariel Shamir, Ohad Fried
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Font Generation
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ndvbd/NIV)
- **Summary**: 将静态字体转换为可变字体需要专家手工操作。本文提出NIV方法，自动预测字形轮廓的点位移，利用属性嵌入处理多轴交互。在Google Fonts数据集上训练，可泛化到未见字符、字体甚至手写输入，生成标准可变字体文件。

<h3 id="132">[132] X-Band UAV-enabled Integrated Sensing and Communications for Vehicular Networks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05262](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05262) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Remon Polus, Soumaya Cherkaoui
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, UAV
- **Summary**: 无人机使能的集成感知与通信系统在智能交通中的应用需要优化时间分配。本文分析感知精度与通信性能的权衡，提出优化框架。仿真结果表明自适应时间分配策略受信道条件和目标距离影响。

<h3 id="133">[133] Policy-Conditioned Counterfactual Credit for Verifiable Reinforcement Learning of Long-Horizon Language Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05263](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05263) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Renwei Meng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Agent, Long-Horizon Planning
- **Summary**: 长程语言智能体存在证据链不可靠和走捷径问题。本文提出CVT-RL算法，结合可验证奖励、干预有效性门控和策略条件反事实贡献估计器。在多个任务上任务成功率从71.8%提升至78.9%，证据F1提高，作弊率下降。

<h3 id="134">[134] Personal AI Agent for Camera Roll VQA</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05275](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05275) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thao Nguyen, Krishna Kumar Singh, Donghyun Kim, Yong Jae Lee, Yuheng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, LLM Personalization, Long Context
- **Summary**: 个人相册视觉问答需要理解长时程个性化视觉内容，缺乏数据集。本文构建camroll数据集和配备分层记忆的camroll-agent。实验表明该智能体优于基线，揭示了长上下文推理在个性化视觉记忆中的挑战。

<h3 id="135">[135] Do Models Share Safety Representations? Cross-Model Steering for Safe Visual Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05290](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05290) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tobia Poppi, Silvia Cappelletti, Sara Sarto, Florian Schiffers, Garin Kessler, Marcella Cornia, Lorenzo Baraldi, Rita Cucchiara
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.MM
- **Tags**: AI Safety, LLM Safety, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 生成模型的安全控制方法通常针对特定模型，需重新训练。本文提出跨模型安全引导框架，在源LLM上估计安全方向，通过轻量对齐迁移到目标生成器，无需目标侧不安全数据。在文本到图像/视频任务上，安全方向迁移的效果与本地学习相当。

<h3 id="136">[136] Agentic Monte Carlo: Simulating Reinforcement Learning for Black-Box Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05296](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05296) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dae Yon Hwang, Raunaq Suri, Valentin Villecroze, Anthony L. Caterini, Jesse C. Cresswell, Noël Vouitsis, Brendan Leigh Ross
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Reinforcement Learning, Test-Time Scaling
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/layer6ai-labs/Agentic-Monte-Carlo)
- **Summary**: 黑盒LLM智能体无法使用强化学习方法。本文提出Agentic Monte Carlo方法，利用序贯蒙特卡洛采样最优策略，通过学习价值函数引导智能体而不改变黑盒模型。在AgentGym基准上优于提示基线甚至GRPO。

<h3 id="137">[137] Statistically Reliable LLM-Based Ranking Evaluation via Prediction-Powered Inference</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05308](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05308) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Abhishek Divekar
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.IR; stat.AP
- **Tags**: LLM-as-a-Judge, LLM Evaluation, Prediction-Powered Inference
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 GEM Workshop
- **Summary**: LLM评估中的排名度量需偏差校正。本文将预测驱动推断扩展到分层指标如Precision@K，结合少量人工标注与大量LLM标注。在ESCI基准上标准误差降低21%，在生产系统中正确识别最优变体并带来销售增长。

<h3 id="138">[138] LoRi: Low-Rank Distillation for Implicit Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05315](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05315) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ryan Solgi, Jiayi Tian, Zheng Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Chain-of-Thought, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 隐式思维链方法通常不如显式思维链。本文提出低秩蒸馏框架，在低秩张量子空间中对齐教师和学生轨迹。在数学推理基准上持续提升性能，接近显式思维链精度。

<h3 id="139">[139] Gradient descent at the Edge of Stability: free energy model and kinetic description of the two-layer network</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05326](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05326) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Antonin Chodron de Courcel
- **Subjects**: math.OC; cs.AI; cs.LG; math.AP
- **Tags**: Optimization, Training Stability, Learning Theory
- **Summary**: 大学习率梯度下降进入边缘稳定状态，产生持续振荡。本文提出连续时间有效模型，结合自由能和曲率熵项，推导双层宽网络的平均场极限。数值实验验证了模型捕捉振荡包络的准确性。

<h3 id="140">[140] The Invisible Hand of Physics: When Video Diffusion Models Know More Than They Show</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05328) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Parsa Esmati, Somjit Nath, Katja Hofmann, Derek Nowrouzezahrai, Samira Ebrahimi Kahou, Majid Mirmehdi
- **Subjects**: cs.GR; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Video Generation, Physical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 视频扩散模型是否内部编码物理结构？本文通过逆向确定性采样恢复潜在轨迹，发现物理合理性可从扩散Transformer状态线性解码（~81%准确率），信号在去噪过程中涌现。

<h3 id="141">[141] A Model of Multi-turn Human Persuadability Using Probabilistic Belief Tracing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05330](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05330) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jared Moore, Noah Goodman, Nick Haber, Max Kleiman-Weiner
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC
- **Tags**: Persuasion, Dialogue System, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 说服研究通常只测量前后信念变化，忽略对话中的动态。本文提出PERSUASIONTRACE框架，包含贝叶斯网络模拟目标。实验发现LLM在多种主题和模态下具有说服力，贝叶斯目标的人类相似度得分（81）接近人类参考（80），远高于基线LLM（64）。

<h3 id="142">[142] A Taxonomy of Runtime Faults in Model Context Protocol Servers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05339](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05339) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Joshua Owotogbe, Indika Kumara, Willem-Jan van den Heuvel, Damian Andrew Tamburri, Antonio Ken Iannillo, Roberto Natella
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, Taxonomy, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: MCP服务器运行时故障特征缺乏实证研究。本文分析837个故障线程，归纳出11大类27子类73叶节点故障类型，并通过开发者调查验证。该分类反映了广泛观察到的运行时故障。

<h3 id="143">[143] Three-Dimensional Retinal Microvasculature Restoration in OCT Angiography</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05375](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05375) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yukun Guo, Min Gao, Tristan T. Hormel, Steven T. Bailey, Thomas S. Hwang, Yali Jia
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical Imaging, Image Restoration, 3D Reconstruction
- **Summary**: OCTA成像伪影影响量化，现有方法忽略3D血管结构。本文提出深度学习算法，使用EfficientNet-B5和挤压激励模块，从相邻B帧恢复中间帧。PSNR和SSIM显著提升，Dice系数在2D和3D分别提高至少3.8%和51.2%。

<h3 id="144">[144] Pattern Selectivity is Not Task-Causal Structure: A Cross-Architecture Mechanistic Study of Composed-Task Circuits in 1B-Class Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05378](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05378) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yongzhong Xu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Circuit Discovery, LLM Reasoning
- **Summary**: 基于模式选择性和因果消融的电路识别方法在不同模型家族中是否产生一致结果？本文在三个1B类模型上四个组合任务测试，发现没有任何两个（任务，模型）单元共享相同的主要因果屏幕。MoE模型基于前一个标记位置子电路。

<h3 id="145">[145] Can AI Refute Economic Theory? Evidence from Beyond the Knowledge Cutoff</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05383](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05383) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexis Akira Toda
- **Subjects**: econ.GN; cs.AI; econ.TH
- **Tags**: Scientific Reasoning, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 该论文探讨了AI是否能反驳经济理论的问题。通过让多个AI模型检查四篇包含已知错误的经济学论文，发现性能最好的ChatGPT Pro偶尔能构建反例和修正证明，但无法在没有大量人类引导的情况下自主发现错误。研究表明，人类与前沿模型协作可以超越当前同行评审，但AI目前无法独立反驳经济理论。

<h3 id="146">[146] Human oversight of agentic systems in practice: Examining the oversight work, challenges, and heuristics of developers using software agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05391](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05391) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shipi Dhanorkar, Samir Passi, Mihaela Vorvoreanu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Human-Computer Interaction, Software Engineering, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了开发者在实际使用自主软件代理时进行的监督工作。通过对17名经验丰富的开发者进行访谈，识别了四种监督形式：先验控制、共同规划、实时监控和事后审查，并记录了监督挑战和应对策略。研究发现监督不仅是被动反应性的，也包括预防性和主动性的工作。

<h3 id="147">[147] VASO: Formally Verifiable Self-Evolving Skills for Physical AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05395](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05395) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yunhao Yang, Neel P. Bhatt, Kevin Wang, Samuel Tetteh, Zhangyang Wang, Ufuk Topcu
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, Formal Methods, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了VASO框架，用于对LLM生成的机器人技能合约进行验证引导的自我演化。每个技能被表示为具有形式化接口和规划器接口的语义合约，通过模型检查器过滤逻辑不一致的合约并验证计划，当验证失败时将反例轨迹转化为文本梯度更新技能合约。在Clearpath Jackal和PX4四旋翼无人机任务上，VASO达到97.2%的形式规范合规率，优于多种基线方法。

<h3 id="148">[148] Willing but Unable: Separating Refusal from Capability in Code LLMs via Abliteration</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05396](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05396) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Cristina Carleo, Pietro Liguori, Naghmeh Ivaki, Domenico Cotroneo
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.SE
- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Safety, Model Editing
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了通过低秩权重编辑（abliteration）去除代码LLM的安全拒绝行为以生成漏洞代码的可行性。以Qwen2.5-Coder-Instruct系列模型和CWE-89（SQL注入）为案例，发现abliteration可将拒绝率降至零，同时保持语法有效性超过93%，且注入率受模型容量限制。研究表明，拒绝与代码生成能力是可分离的。

<h3 id="149">[149] ReasoningFlow: Discourse Structures for Understanding LLM Reasoning Traces</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05402](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05402) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jinu Lee, Shivam Agarwal, Amruta Parulekar, Siddarth Madala, Dilek Hakkani-Tur, Julia Hockenmaier
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Discourse Segmentation, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/jinulee-v/reasoningflow)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了ReasoningFlow框架，将大型推理模型的推理轨迹捕捉为细粒度的有向无环图（DAG）。通过人工标注和自动标注，分析了1260条推理轨迹，发现不同模型的推理结构相似，大多数错误步骤不用于推导最终答案，且语言层面的语篇结构不反映机械因果依赖。

<h3 id="150">[150] Trust, but Don't Verify: Epistemic Blind Spots in LLM Source Evaluation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05403](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05403) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rohan N. Pradhan, Steve Goley
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Fact Checking, LLM Evaluation, Misinformation
- **Summary**: 该论文揭示了LLM在多源综合时不能有效评估证据质量的问题。模型能够检测虚假统计（正确识别率0.76-1.00），但在多源综合中不会利用这一能力，而是根据方法论风格表面特征赋予权重，导致统计上不可能的置信区间得到相同权重。机制分析表明，数值有效性信号在多源综合中被抑制。

<h3 id="151">[151] CausalPOI: Spatio-Temporal Graph-Based Causal Modeling for Cold-Start POI Check-in Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05413](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05413) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhaoqi Zhang, Miao Xie, Yi Li, Linyou Cai, Siqiang Luo, Gao Cong
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Spatiotemporal Forecasting, Causal Inference, Graph Neural Network
- **Venue**: KDD 2026
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了冷启动POI签到预测问题，并设计了CausalPOI框架，利用时空功能交互图建模POI之间的语义和空间关系，并通过结构对齐的处理和对照图模拟事实和反事实场景。在真实SafeGraph数据集上，CausalPOI显著优于最先进的基线，提高了时空预测、语义交互建模和因果效应估计的性能。

<h3 id="152">[152] When Evidence is Sparse: Weakly Supervised Early Failure Alerting in Dialogs and LLM-Agent Trajectories</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05414](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05414) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Avinash Baidya, Xinran Liang, Ruocheng Guo, Xiang Gao, Kamalika Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.HC; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Dialogue System, LLM Agent, Failure Detection
- **Summary**: 该论文针对对话和代理轨迹中的早期失败预警问题，提出了一种两阶段方法。首先通过注意力机制从轨迹标签中学习稀疏的逐轮失败证据，然后使用α-STOP策略在推理时选择准确率-及时性平衡点。在多个基准上，该方法改进了帕累托前沿质量，并降低了训练成本。

<h3 id="153">[153] Executable Schema Contracts: From Automatic Ingestion to Multi-Source Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05415](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05415) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Padmaja Jonnalagedda, Yuguang Yao, Xiang Gao, Hilaf Hasson, Kamalika Das
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, RAG, Information Retrieval
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一个从多源数据自动发现可执行模式（schema）的系统，并用于知识图谱构建和查询时检索。通过闭合世界字段目录约束LLM的模式发现，使用确定性结构分析推断键和层次结构，并利用模式引导多工具代理进行检索。在零样本对比中，该方法优于纯检索和分解基线。

<h3 id="154">[154] Selective-Advantage Entropy-Adaptive Horizon GRPO: Asymmetric Token-Level Discounting for Efficient Reinforcement Learning of Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05434](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05434) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Chirag Chawla, Rohan Charudatt Salvi, Madhav S. Baidya
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Mathematical Reasoning
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了选择性优势熵自适应视野GRPO（SA-AH-GRPO），在GRPO基础上引入基于熵的折扣和不对称机制：仅对负优势轨迹进行折扣。在GSM8K数学推理基准上，SA-AH-GRPO在3B模型上达到Pass@1=0.858，训练方差降低3.6倍，同时保持峰值准确率。

<h3 id="155">[155] GOTabPFN: From Feature Ordering to Compact Tokenization for Tabular Foundation Models on High-Dimensional Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05441](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05441) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Al Zadid Sultan Bin Habib, Md Younus Ahamed, Prashnna Kumar Gyawali, Gianfranco Doretto, Donald A. Adjeroh
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; stat.ML
- **Tags**: Tabular Learning, Foundation Model
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/zadid6pretam/GOTabPFN;)
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了GOTabPFN，通过图引导排序和局部细化（GO-LR）以及神经启发子单元压缩（NSC），使小型表格基础模型在高维低样本量（HDLSS）表格预测中有效。在表格基准上，GOTabPFN在紧凑的token预算下提高了稳定性和准确性。

<h3 id="156">[156] Multilingual Coreference Resolution via Cycle-Consistent Machine Translation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05444](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05444) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Adriana-Valentina Costache, Eduard Poesina, Silviu-Florin Gheorghe, Paul Irofti, Radu Tudor Ionescu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Coreference Resolution, Machine Translation, Low-Resource NLP
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了一种基于循环一致机器翻译的多语言共指消解流水线，通过从英语翻译到目标低资源语言生成训练数据，并利用反向翻译的余弦相似度进行质量验证。在四种低资源语言上的实验表明，该方法带来了显著的性能提升，且能够实现之前没有语料的语言的共指消解。

<h3 id="157">[157] Towards Unified and Data-Efficient Prognostics and Health Management with Tabular Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Raffael Theiler, Lev Telyatnikov, Leandro Von Krannichfeldt, Olga Fink
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SP
- **Tags**: Prognostics and Health Management, Tabular Learning, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了将表格基础模型应用于工业时间序列的框架，使用上下文学习实现预测和健康管理（PHM）任务。通过将单位级信号转换为表格行，模型在多个PHM任务（预测和诊断）上表现良好，且数据效率高。与序列模型和梯度提升树相比，表格基础模型在低数据场景下具有竞争力。

<h3 id="158">[158] MASF: A Multi-Model Adaptive Selection Framework for Abstractive Text summarization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05494](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05494) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Alansary, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Summarization, Model Selection
- **Summary**: 该论文提出了多模型自适应摘要框架（MASF），集成多个微调的Transformer模型，并通过自动评估指标选择最佳摘要。在CNN/DailyMail数据集上，MASF取得了最高的BERTScore（88.63%），优于GPT3-D2等LLM，展示了鲁棒性和有效性。

<h3 id="159">[159] The Role of Instructional Guidance in Generative AI-Assisted Learning: Empirical Evidence from Construction Engineering Education</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05509](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05509) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xiaoyu Hou, Bo Xiao, Hexu Liu, Shane Mueller
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Education Technology, Prompt Engineering, Human-Computer Interaction
- **Summary**: 该论文研究了教学指导在生成式AI辅助学习中的作用，在建筑教育中引入五步提示框架。控制实验比较了幻灯片学习、无提示AI学习和有提示AI学习三种条件。结果表明，有提示条件在开放式任务上表现更好（提高2-3分，p\<0.01），说明AI辅助学习的有效性取决于交互结构。

<h3 id="160">[160] Exploring LLMs for South Asian Music Understanding and Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05522](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05522) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Faria Binte Kader, Mohtasim Hadi Rafi, Shah Wasif Sajjad, Santu Karmaker
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Music Generation, LLM Evaluation, Audio-Language Model
- **Summary**: 该论文首次系统评估了LLM在南亚古典音乐（如印度斯坦古典音乐和孟加拉古典音乐）上的理解和生成能力。引入了504个问答基准，前沿模型如Gemini 2.5 Pro达到85-90%准确率，而开源模型多为23-40%。在音乐生成方面，即使最强模型也只有40%的时间产生风格一致的输出。

<h3 id="161">[161] Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench: A Bilingual Multimodal Benchmark for Cognitively Informed Evaluation of Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05531](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05531) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Mahdi Abootorabi, Omid Ghahroodi, Anas Madkoor, Marzia Nouri, Doratossadat Dastgheib, Mohamed Hefeeda, Ehsaneddin Asgari
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Benchmark
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Findings
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/qcri/Almieyar-Oryx-BloomBench)
- **Summary**: 现有视觉语言模型（VLM）基准缺乏对真正推理能力的严格诊断。本文提出BloomBench，首个基于Bloom认知分类学的双语多模态基准，系统评估六个认知层次。研究发现顶尖模型在语义理解上表现强，但在事实回忆和创造性综合上显著不足，且阿拉伯语性能逊于英语。

<h3 id="162">[162] What Objects Enable, Not What They Are: Functional Latent Spaces for Affordance Reasoning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05533](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05533) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rohan Siva, Neel P. Bhatt, Yunhao Yang, Seoyoung Lee, Nishant Gadde, Christian Ellis, Alvaro Velasquez, Zhangyang Wang, Ufuk Topcu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Affordance Learning
- **Summary**: 机器人规划依赖外观推理，难以泛化至新交互。本文提出A4D，将视觉观察映射到功能潜在空间（affordance），并引入新affordance发现机制。A4D在多个规划任务中达到94%推理准确率，超过SOTA 15%以上，新affordance推理从70%提升至90%+，推理速度提升100倍。

<h3 id="163">[163] Noise-Aware Visual Representation Learning for Medical Visual Question Answering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05535](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05535) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: I Putu Adi Pratama, Bahadorreza Ofoghi, Atul Sajjanhar, Shang Gao
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical VQA
- **Summary**: 医学视觉问答（Med-VQA）常忽略视觉表示中的噪声问题。本文提出噪声感知框架，包含去噪自编码器，并结合LoRA微调。在SLAKE和PathVQA上方法提高了鲁棒性，同时保持竞争性清洁性能。

<h3 id="164">[164] ADK Arena: Evaluating Agent Development Kits via LLM-as-a-Developer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05548](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05548) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jintao Huang, Xiaomin Li, Gaurav Mittal, Yu Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Benchmark
- **Summary**: 代理开发工具包（ADK）数量激增但缺乏效果实证。本文提出LLM-as-Developer方法，用LLM编码代理学习API、编写代码并迭代修复，构建ADK Arena自动化评估流水线。评估51个框架发现生成成功率57%，成本差异5.6倍，最佳框架解决80%任务。

<h3 id="165">[165] Conformal Risk-Averse Decision Making with Action Conditional Guarantee</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05551](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05551) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zihan Zhu, Shayan Kiyani, George Pappas. Hamed Hassani
- **Subjects**: stat.ML; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Conformal Prediction, Uncertainty Estimation
- **Summary**: 现有共形预测提供边际安全保证，而非行动条件保证。本文提出行动条件共形预测，扩展至风险厌恶决策，并基于pinball损失最小化算法。在真实数据集上显著改进行动条件性能。

<h3 id="166">[166] Balancing Image Compression and Generation with Bootstrapped Tokenization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05552](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05552) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haozhe Chi, Jinghan Li, Hao Jiang, Wu Sheng, Yi Ma, Jing Wang, Yadong Mu
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GR
- **Tags**: Image Tokenization, Image Generation
- **Summary**: 标准图像tokenization混合不同粒度信息导致冗余。本文提出SelfBootTok，通过自引导学习将信息分解为全局和局部token组，生成器仅需全局token。方法计算减少约40%，gFID达1.56，仅用64个token。

<h3 id="167">[167] ArcANE: Do Role-Playing Language Agents Stay in Character at the Right Time?</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05553](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05553) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Woojung Song, Nalim Kim, Sangjun Song, Chaewon Heo, Jongwon Lim, Yohan Jo
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Role-Playing, Narrative Understanding
- **Summary**: 角色扮演语言代理应随故事演变，现有基准仅测事实回忆。本文提出ArcANE基准，基于角色弧分段叙事，评估相同场景在不同阶段的反应。以角色弧为条件的策略在所有模型上表现最佳，尤其对文本外场景。

<h3 id="168">[168] Representation Learning Enables Scalable Multitask Deep Reinforcement Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05555](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05555) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Johan Obando-Ceron, Lu Li, Scott Fujimoto, Pierre-Luc Bacon, Aaron Courville, Pablo Samuel Castro
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Representation Learning, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 多任务RL中模型基控制复杂，关键组件不明。本文认为表示学习是主要驱动力，提出简单无模型算法MR.Q结合预测辅助目标。方法超越世界模型方法和多种baseline，显著降低计算开销。

<h3 id="169">[169] InfoShield: Privacy-Preserving Speech Representations for Mental Health Screening via Information-Theoretic Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05561](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05561) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xueyang Wu, Siyuan Liu, Kezhuo Yang, Guang Ling
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Privacy, Mental Health, Speech Processing
- **Summary**: 语音心理健康筛查面临隐私问题。本文提出InfoShield，最小化语音表示与敏感属性的互信息，同时保持抑郁分类准确率；提出TimeAwareMINE解决时序-静态错位。性别推断从92.6%降至55.5%，年龄推断从55.7%降至30.3%，F1=0.784。

<h3 id="170">[170] TensorBench: Benchmarking Coding Agents on a Compiler-Based Tensor Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05570](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05570) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Bobby Yan, Fredrik Kjolstad
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Repo-Level Code Generation, Agent Evaluation
- **Summary**: 仓库级编码基准在难度和评估可靠性间存在权衡。本文引入TensorBench，199个任务针对编译器张量框架，通过测试套件自动评分。最佳代理通过率64.8%，最弱22.1%，两最强代理一致性低（κ=0.05）。

<h3 id="171">[171] Dimensionality Reduction for Cyberattack Classification: A Comparative Evaluation of PCA and Linear Predictive Coding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05584](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05584) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Nelly Elsayed, Zag ElSayed, Navid Asadizanjani
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Intrusion Detection, Dimensionality Reduction
- **Summary**: 高维特征用于网络攻击检测增加计算复杂度。本文比较PCA和LPC两种降维技术，实验表明PCA在强压缩下保持分类性能，LPC性能略差但仍有竞争力。

<h3 id="172">[172] HDST-GNN: Heterogeneous Dynamic Spatiotemporal Graph Neural Networks for Multi-Object Tracking in UAV Aerial Imagery</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05587](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05587) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Phillip Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Object Tracking, Graph Neural Network, Remote Sensing
- **Summary**: UAV多目标跟踪面临高度变化、小物体密集、遮挡问题。本文提出HDST-GNN，异构动态时空图神经网络，包含高度自适应边构建、异构节点表示、遮挡门控时间聚合。在VisDrone2019-MOT上MOTA 94.51%，IDF1 97.24%，ID切换减少81%。

<h3 id="173">[173] Cross-Epoch Adaptive Rollout Optimization for RL Post-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05606](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05606) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yiming Zong, Yige Wang, Jiashuo Jiang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; math.OC
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: LLM后训练中固定rollout预算未考虑不同prompt训练信号差异。本文提出CERO，基于贝叶斯后验自适应分配rollout，使用Fenchel对偶重构成在线资源分配问题。在数学推理问题上一致超越GRPO。

<h3 id="174">[174] The End of Software Engineering: How AI Agents Are Fundamentally Restructuring the Software Paradigm</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05608](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05608) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhenfeng Cao
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Software Engineering, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: AI代理的出现从根本上重构软件范式。本文从第一性原理分析复杂性扩展，形式化传统软件与代理系统的区别，提出Agentic Engineering新学科。通过基准证据展示代理范式的变革潜力与局限，提出四阶段路线图。

<h3 id="175">[175] SlotGCG: Exploiting the Positional Vulnerability in LLMs for Jailbreak Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05609](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05609) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seungwon Jeong, Jiwoo Jeong, Hyeonjin Kim, Yunseok Lee, Woojin Lee
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Jailbreak Attack, LLM Security
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/youai058/SlotGCG)
- **Summary**: GCG攻击将对抗token固定在prompt后缀，未探索其他位置。本文提出SlotGCG，评估所有slot的脆弱性得分，选择最脆弱的slot插入token进行优化攻击。ASR提高14%，收敛更快，对防御方法鲁棒性提升42%。

<h3 id="176">[176] When New Generators Arrive: Lifelong Machine-Generated Text Attribution via Ridge Feature Transfer</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05626](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05626) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhen Sun, Yifan Liao, Zhicong Huang, Jiaheng Wei, Cheng Hong, Yutao Yue, Xinlei He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Continual Learning
- **Summary**: 终生机器生成文本归因需持续加入新生成器而保持旧类识别。本文提出RidgeFT，轻量分析更新框架，无需示例重放，使用统计量和岭回归闭式更新。在多主题评估中一致优于baseline，提高旧类保留和新类适应。

<h3 id="177">[177] Enhancing Software Engineering Through Closed-Loop Memory Optimization</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05646](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05646) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xuehang Guo, Zora Zhiruo Wang, Qingyun Wang, Graham Neubig, Xingyao Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 解决LLM软件工程代理缺乏跨任务经验保留和复用的问题。提出闭环记忆优化框架，将记忆效用定义为验证过的下游影响，作为评估基准和优化信号。在单一和跨片段评估中，成功率和解决效率分别提升5.25%和4.63%，计算成本降低9.79%以上。

<h3 id="178">[178] When Surface Form Changes Moderation Decisions: A Paired Study of Code-Mixed Workflow Instability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05654](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05654) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Suraj Babu Thimma Krishnaram
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Content Moderation, Code-Switching, Hate Speech Detection
- **Summary**: 研究混合语言输入对仇恨言论审核工作流的影响，发现英文输入到泰米尔语-英语混合语言输入时决策翻转率高达0.265，审核负担增加。提出基于分歧的降级规则可减少错误但增加审核量。说明工作流级评估能揭示标准分类无法发现的失败。

<h3 id="179">[179] Agent-Orchestrated Adaptive RAG: A Comparative Study on Structured and Multi-Hop Retrieval</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05658](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05658) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anuj Maharjan, Devinder Kaur, Richard Molyet
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, LLM Agent, Multi-Hop Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对传统RAG在复杂查询上的局限，提出代理协调的自适应RAG框架，包含动态查询分解和迭代检索。在结构化领域（DevOps）中查询分解带来+0.04整体得分和+0.17 MRR提升，但在多跳基准上反而降低排名精度。反思机制提升引用准确性但带来延迟成本，表明代理增强需要选择性应用。

<h3 id="180">[180] Safe Embodied AI for Long-horizon Tasks: A Cross-layer Analysis of Robotic Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05660](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05660) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dabin Kim, Daemin Park, Sangyub Lee, Jinsik Kim, Yeongtak Oh, Jongho Shin, Sungroh Yoon
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Safe RL, Embodied AI
- **Summary**: 综述长时域机器人操作中的安全问题，从规划时、策略时和执行时三个干预点组织文献。识别出策略时安全证据不足、接触丰富操作缺乏形式化保证、不确定性触发干预不成熟等空白。为跨层保障和评估设计提供研究方向。

<h3 id="181">[181] LongSpace: Exploring Long-Horizon Spatial Memory from Perception to Recall in Video</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05677](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05677) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shiqiang Lang, Jing Liu, Haoyang He, Peiwen Sun, Yuanteng Chen, Tao Liu, Lan Yang, Longteng Guo, Honggang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Video Understanding, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 针对多模态大模型在长视频空间记忆上的不足，提出LongSpace-Bench基准和LongSpace记忆框架。LongSpace将长视频分块、注入3D结构线索并构建分层记忆用于问题引导检索。实验表明显式空间记忆是长视频MLLM的关键能力。

<h3 id="182">[182] Beyond Waveform Robustness: Robust Feature-Vocoder Adversarial Attacks on Automatic Speech Recognition</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05678](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05678) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yifan Liao, Zongmin Zhang, Zhen Sun, Yuhui Sun, Xinhu Zheng, Xinlei He
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; cs.CR
- **Tags**: Automatic Speech Recognition, Adversarial Robustness, Black-Box Attack
- **Summary**: 针对ASR对抗攻击迁移性差和易被防御的问题，提出基于Clean-Referenced Feature-Vocoder的攻击方法。将对抗搜索空间从原始波形转移到SSL表征空间，通过声码器重构为类语音信号。在黑盒模型上WER提升+26.6，对多种防御提升+36.2，揭示ASR鲁棒性评估的盲点。

<h3 id="183">[183] Data Flow Control: Data Safety Policies for AI Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05679](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05679) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Charlie Summers, Eugene Wu
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Data Privacy, Database, AI Agent
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/dataflowcontrol/data-flow-control)
- **Summary**: 针对AI代理生成SQL和自动化数据分析时的数据安全问题，提出数据流控制（DFC）框架。通过Passant查询重写层强制执行元组级数据流策略，无需物化溯源。在五个DBMS引擎上几乎零开销，比替代方案快几个数量级。

<h3 id="184">[184] Value-and-Structure Alignment for Routing-Consistent Quantization of Mixture-of-Experts Models</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对MoE模型量化导致的专家路由不稳定问题，提出VSRAQ后训练量化目标。通过值对齐和结构对齐联合保留路由行为，不引入推理开销。实验表明VSRAQ提升专家选择一致性，优于仅重建和路由感知基线。

<h3 id="185">[185] Benchmarking Counterfactual Prediction in Epidemic Time Series with Time-Varying Interventions</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05692](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05692) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="186">[186] Cognitive Threat Intelligence and Explainable Federated Security Analytics for distributed Infrastructure Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05701](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05701) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="187">[187] Explainable AI-Driven Cyber Risk Analytics and Model Reliability Assessment for Intelligent Governance of U.S. Critical Infrastructure: An XGBoost and SHAP-Based Intrusion Detection Framework</h3>

- **Summary**: 针对美国关键基础设施的网络安全治理，提出基于XGBoost和SHAP的入侵检测与风险分析框架。使用多种分类器检测恶意活动，集成XAI增强透明度。在CICIDS2017数据集上评估准确率、精确率等指标。

<h3 id="188">[188] ViCuR: Visual Cues as Recoverable Privilege for Multimodal On-Policy Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05718](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05718) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Kanghui Tian, Siyuan Liu, Ziang Yan, Sheng Xia, Shuai Dong, Yi Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Knowledge Distillation, Vision-Language Model
- **Summary**: 针对多模态推理中基于答案特权的师生蒸馏导致的捷径模仿问题，提出ViCuR框架。用视觉线索替代答案特权，通过轻量线索恢复模块在预填充时聚合证据。在七个基准上，2B和8B模型平均提升+1.19和+1.24，且可扩展至更强教师。

<h3 id="189">[189] Microskill Architecture: A Modular Skill-Driven Framework for AI-Native Code Generation</h3>

- **Tags**: Code Generation, LLM Agent, Software Engineering
- **Summary**: 针对AI原生代码生成中上下文窗口管理问题，提出微技能架构（MicroSkill Architecture）。将知识封装为原子化的技能胶囊，通过动态路由器选择相关胶囊。在案例分析中，令牌消耗降低90%以上，首次编译成功率翻倍，消除架构违规。

<h3 id="190">[190] Narrative Knowledge Weaver: Narrative-Centric Retrieval-Augmented Reasoning for Long-Form Text Understanding</h3>

- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 针对长文本叙事问答中证据依赖故事世界的问题，提出叙事知识编织器（NKW）。将文本证据、原子事实、图结构、实体画像等对齐，在查询时使用文本、图、叙事工具组装证据。在STAGE等基准上，剧本级问答表现最强，并在多个推理维度上互补。

<h3 id="191">[191] Let It Be Simple: One-Step Action Generation for Vision-Language-Action Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05737](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05737) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yitong Chen, Shiduo Zhang, Jingjing Gong, Xipeng Qiu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.RO
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Diffusion Model, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 针对扩散VLA模型动作生成依赖迭代去噪的问题，提出简单的一步动作生成方法。仅通过偏向高噪声状态的训练时间分布，无需教师模型或蒸馏。一步策略匹配十步解码，在LIBERO-Long上达95.6%，表明一步生成可从标准扩散训练实现。

<h3 id="192">[192] UNIVID: Unified Vision-Language Model for Video Moderation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05748](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05748) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Content Moderation, Video Understanding
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Industry Track
- **Summary**: 针对全球视频审核需要细粒度多模态推理和可解释性的问题，提出统一视觉语言模型UNIVID。生成策略感知字幕作为可解释中间表示，结合专家标注和合成数据对齐安全指南。集成后违规泄漏减少42.7%，过杀率减少37.0%，替换1000多个模型。

<h3 id="193">[193] MARDoc: A Memory-Aware Refinement Agent Framework for Multimodal Long Document QA</h3>

- **Summary**: 现有迭代检索-推理代理在多模态长文档问答中混合上下文导致关键证据分散。本文提出MARDoc，包含探索器、精炼器和反射器三个专用代理，利用动态结构化记忆解耦文档QA。在MMLongBench-Doc和DocBench上超越同骨干基线，证明了结构化记忆的有效性。

<h3 id="194">[194] SagnacAssisted Enhanced OTDR for Distributed Acoustic Sensing: A Standardized Benchmark and Engineering Evaluation Framework</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05754](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05754) (cross-listed)

<h3 id="195">[195] Beyond Soft Masks: Hard-Perturbation Mixup Explainer for Robust GNN Explainability</h3>

<h3 id="196">[196] DRIFT: A Residual Flow Adapter for Decoding Continuous Outputs in Vision-Language Models</h3>

<h3 id="198">[198] An Improved CNN-LSTM Based Intrusion Detection System for IoT Networks</h3>

<h3 id="199">[199] TinyML-Driven Cybersecurity for Autonomous Spacecraft: Latency-Accuracy Analysis for SPARTA RF and Cyber Threat Detection</h3>

<h3 id="201">[201] CollabBench: Benchmarking and Unleashing Collaborative Ability of LLMs with Diverse Players via Proactive Engagement</h3>

- **Summary**: LLM代理与人类合作仍具挑战。本文提出CollabBench基准，包含多样化玩家模拟和协作代理训练范式，统一推理、通信和行动。训练模型在效率和情感指标上分别提升19.5%和24.4%。

<h3 id="202">[202] Emotion-Aware Image Generation from Korean Diary Text via LLM-based Prompt Translation and LoRA Fine-Tuning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05816](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05816) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jihun Cho, Soo-Yeon Jeong, Sun-Young Ihm
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Text-to-Image, Emotion Recognition, LoRA
- **Summary**: 文生图模型难以从日记文本捕捉情感。本文提出情感感知管道，使用Qwen3-8B识别情感，LoRA微调Stable Diffusion 3.5生成儿童手绘风格图像。实验分析情感触发词效果，讨论CLIP分数局限。

<h3 id="203">[203] Consistency Training Along the Transformer Stack</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05817](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05817) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sukrati Gautam, Neil Shah, Arav Dhoot, Bryan Maruyama, Caroline Wei, Rohan Kapoor, Robert Sidey, Prakhar Gupta, Zi Cheng Huang, David Demitri Africa
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Alignment, LLM Safety, Consistency Training
- **Summary**: 一致性训练可减少幻觉和越狱。本文引入MLP和注意力一致性训练目标，应用于四种安全威胁。结果表明一致性训练减少失调，具有跨威胁泛化能力，区分不同机制。

<h3 id="204">[204] Benchmarks in Leipzig</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05818](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05818) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Andrei Balakin, Miklós Bóna, Marie-Charlotte Brandenburg, Clara Briand, Veronica Calvo Cortes, Shelby Cox, Jesus A. De Loera, Danai Deligeorgaki, Hannah Friedman, Tim Gehrunger, Chiara Giardino, Stephen Griffeth, Baran Hashemi, Elena Hoster, Alexander Ivanov, Nupur Jain, Aryaman Jal, Leonie Kayser, Joris Koefler, Kevin Kühn, Mario Kummer, Felix Lotter, René Marczinzik, Victor S. Miller, Alejandro Morales, Greta Panova, Gianni Petrella, Nathan Pflueger, Lakshmi Ramesh, Nikolas Rieke, Carlos Rodriguez, Andrea Rosana, Flavio Salizzoni, Otto T.P. Schmidt, Sven Ulf Schmitz, Lina Maria Simbaqueba Marin, Luca Sodomaco, Christian Stump, Bernd Sturmfels, Alexander Taveira Blomenhofer, Simon Telen, Philipp Tuchel, Emil Verkama, Carl Felix Waller, Julian Weigert, Annette Werner, Nathan Williams, Claudius Zibrowius
- **Subjects**: math.HO; cs.AI; math.AG; math.CO; math.RT

<h3 id="205">[205] Learning Geometric Representations from Videos for Spatial Intelligent Multimodal Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05833](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05833) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haibo Wang, Lifu Huang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, 3D Vision, Spatial Reasoning
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型缺乏3D空间意识。本文提出GeoVR，利用2D视频蒸馏3D几何知识，重塑MLLM表示。通过多目标学习(相机姿态、深度、尺度、3D特征)实现强3D感知，在空间推理上达到最先进。

<h3 id="206">[206] Mechanistic Insights into Functional Sparsity in Multimodal LLMs via CoRe Heads</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05843](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05843) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ruoxi Sun, Quantong Qiu, Juntao Li, Zecheng Tang, Yihang Lou, Min Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Vision-Language Model, Sparse Attention
- **Summary**: 多模态大语言模型跨模态检索机制不透明。本文通过检索注意力质量识别上下文感知检索头(CoRe)，发现功能性稀疏。去除前5%CoRe头显著降低推理性能，利用此稀疏性可加速推理。

<h3 id="207">[207] GenTI: Benchmarking LLMs for Autonomous IDPS Rule Generation for Unseen Attacks</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05844](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05844) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hassan Jalil Hadi, Rehana Yasmin, Ali Shoker
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Cybersecurity, Intrusion Detection, Benchmark
- **Summary**: 规则型入侵检测系统难以适应未知攻击。本文提出GenTI基准和LLM管道，自动生成IDPS规则。在15万条规则上，生成规则质量评分89.4%，CTI覆盖率94.8%，未知攻击检测率从45%升至87.4%，误报率从8.5%降至2.3%。

<h3 id="208">[208] UniVoice: A Unified Model for Speech and Singing Voice Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05852](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05852) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Junjie Zheng, Huixin Xue, Shihong Ren, Chaofan Ding, Hao Liu, Zihao Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Text-to-Speech, Singing Voice Synthesis, Audio Generation
- **Summary**: 语音和歌声生成需求不同。本文提出UniVoice，基于条件流匹配，分解内容、旋律和音色条件，共享DiT骨干。对歌声使用MIDI，对语音使用空旋律令牌。30k小时语音和35k小时歌声训练，语音PER 5.26%，歌声PER 16.22%，优于统一基线。

<h3 id="209">[209] EEGDancer: Dynamic Emotion Latent Space Masked Modeling with Reinforcement Learning for EEG Continuous Emotion Prediction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05855](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05855) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhihao Zhou, Weishan Ye, Li Zhang, Gan Huang, Zhen Liang
- **Subjects**: cs.HC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Affective Computing, EEG Analysis, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 现有方法主要依赖逐点回归和直接建模噪声高维EEG特征，难以捕捉连续情绪动态。提出EEGDancer框架，结合向量量化表示学习、掩码时序建模和基于SAC的轨迹优化。在SEED、SEED-IV和长期自然情绪数据集上持续优于现有方法。

<h3 id="210">[210] LLMCodec: Adapting Video Codecs for Efficient Weight Compression of Large Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05861](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05861) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Rui Wang, Yan Zhao, Li Song, Zhengxue Cheng
- **Subjects**: cs.MM; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Model Compression, Quantization, LLM Inference
- **Summary**: 现有模型压缩方法依赖微调或校准数据，泛化性有限。提出LLMCodec，将仿射量化与VVC/H.266视频编码器结合。在LLaMA-3-8B上2-bit精度下困惑度降低1.5倍，下游任务准确率提升21%。

<h3 id="211">[211] Deciphering Two Training Clocks in Grokking via Deep Linear Network Theory with Conditional ReLU Reduction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05863](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05863) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hu Tan, Kuo Gai, Shihua Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Grokking, Deep Learning Theory, Mechanistic Interpretability
- **Summary**: Grokking现象中拟合训练数据与学习简单规则发生在不同时间尺度。通过深度线性网络理论和条件ReLU约化，形式化了两个训练时钟，分离了拟合与表示简化。在模块加法实验上验证。

<h3 id="212">[212] Compositional Boundaries for Density Fusion</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05871](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05871) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ratan Bahadur Thapa, Ali Darijani, Jürgen Beyerer, Steffen Staab
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI; stat.ME
- **Tags**: Probabilistic Inference, Bayesian Inference
- **Summary**: 分布式不确定性管理系统中的密度融合需满足顺序无关性。研究了局部段值融合规则的代数组合边界，证明归一化加权线性池是顺序无关层次化执行的特征。揭示了端点对候选f-散度平衡的局部障碍。

<h3 id="213">[213] LadderMan: Learning Humanoid Perceptive Ladder Climbing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05873](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05873) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Siheng Zhao, Yuanhang Zhang, Ziqi Lu, Pieter Abbeel, Rocky Duan, Koushil Sreenath, Yue Wang, C. Karen Liu, Guanya Shi
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robot, Robot Manipulation, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 人形机器人攀爬梯子因稀疏抓点和全身协调极具挑战。提出LadderMan系统，采用两阶段学习流水线，通过混合运动追踪和知识蒸馏学习攀爬策略，并利用视觉基础模型缩小sim-to-real差距。在多种梯子上实现鲁棒攀爬并零样本迁移到真实硬件。

<h3 id="214">[214] Staying with the Uncertainty: Uncertainty-Scaffolding Strategies for Artificial Moral Advisors in LLM-to-LLM Simulated Conversations</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05890](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05890) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Salvatore Greco, Hainiu Xu, Jacopo Domenicucci, Yulan He, Sylvie Delacroix
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, AI Ethics, Role-Playing
- **Summary**: LLM作为人工道德顾问需选择合适的对话模式。提出三种不确定性策略（观点倍增、张力保持、过程反思）并与控制条件比较。发现不确定性策略在立场修正量上无差异，但在参与质量上不同。

<h3 id="215">[215] Reducing Hallucinations in Complex Question Answering using Simple Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (long version)</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05901](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05901) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Christopher J. Wedge, Joshua Stutter, Danny Dixon, Jacek Cała
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: RAG, Graph Neural Network, Question Answering
- **Summary**: LLM在复杂问答中易产生幻觉。提出基于轻量图结构的RAG系统，结合向量搜索和图查询工具。在MoNaCo基准上显著提高事实正确性的精确率和召回率，幻觉答案减半。

<h3 id="216">[216] Better Literary Translation: A Multi-Aspect Data Generation and LLM Training Approach</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05924](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05924) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zhihao Lin, Ziqi Zhu, Hao Huang, Guanghui Wang, Peiyang He
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Machine Translation, Instruction Tuning, Reinforcement Learning

- **Summary**: 文学翻译缺乏高质量标注数据，需平衡流畅性和文学效果。提出多视角迭代优化框架生成高质量参考和偏好数据，用于SFT和RL。生成的参考在SFT上比原始地面真值高8.65 CEA100，GRPO进一步提升1.51分。

<h3 id="217">[217] To Be Multimodal or Not to Be: Query-Adaptive Audio-Visual Person Retrieval via Active Modality Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05931](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05931) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Erfan Loweimi, Mengjie Qian, Kate Knill, Guanfeng Wu, Chi-Ho Chan, Abbas Haider, Muhammad Awan, Josef Kittler, Hui Wang, Mark Gales

- **Summary**: 真实广播档案中目标可能只出现在音频或视频中，融合缺失模态会引入噪声。提出查询自适应框架，通过跨模态分数一致性检测活跃模态。在BBC Rewind语料上达到94.2% P@1，优于单模态和固定融合。

<h3 id="218">[218] Learning of Robot Safety Policies via Adversarial Synthetic Scenarios</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05952](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05952) (cross-listed)

- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI

- **Summary**: 机器人安全策略学习需发现高风险边缘案例。提出对抗性游戏化框架，红队探索失败场景，蓝队优化安全策略。提供了问题形式化和解决方案架构。

<h3 id="219">[219] Causal Scaffolding for Physical Reasoning: A Benchmark for Causally-Informed Physical World Understanding in VLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05966](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05966) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianyi Tang, Zhuoyi Lin, Zeyu Feng, Tianyi Ma, Yew-Soon Ong, Ivor Tsang, Haiyan Yin

- **Tags**: Causal Reasoning, Vision-Language Model, Benchmark
- **Venue**: KDD 2026 Dataset and Benchmark Track
- **Summary**: 现有VLM在物理因果推理上表现不佳。提出CausalPhys基准（3000+问题带因果图）和CRFT微调方法。显著提升推理准确性和可解释性。

<h3 id="220">[220] Measuring the sensitivity of LLM-based structured extraction to prompt, model, and schema choices in clinical discharge summaries</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05970](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05970) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Martin Murin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: LLM Evaluation, Medical AI, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: LLM在临床文本结构化提取中对配置选择的敏感性未被充分研究。通过保持任务固定、一次改变一个选择来测量敏感性。发现跨提示一致性中值kappa约0.69，模型选择对多类分类影响大于提示措辞。

<h3 id="221">[221] World-Language-Action Model for Unified World Modeling, Language Reasoning, and Action Synthesis</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05979](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05979) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yi Yang, Zhihong Liu, Siqi Kou, Yiyang Chen, Yanzhe Hu, Jianbo Zhou, Boyuan Zhao, Zhijie Wei, Xiao Xia, Xueqi Li, Pengfei Liu, Zhijie Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Embodied AI, World Model, Robot Manipulation
- **Summary**: 现有基础模型未能统一世界建模、语言推理和动作合成。提出WLA模型，使用自回归Transformer预测下一状态（文本意图和物理动态）。在模拟和真实环境中实现SOTA多任务和长时域学习能力。

<h3 id="222">[222] AttackPathGNN: Cross-function vulnerability detection in smart contracts using state interference graphs and conjunction pooling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05986](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05986) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gabriela Dobrita, Simona-Vasilica Oprea, Adela Bara
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Smart Contract, Vulnerability Detection, Graph Neural Network
- **Summary**: 现有智能合约漏洞检测局限于单函数模式匹配。提出AttackPathGNN，通过状态干扰图和合取池化进行跨函数漏洞检测。在SmartBugs上F1达92.3%，6/10 DASP10类别100%召回。

<h3 id="223">[223] Deep Learning-based 3D Oral Cavity Reconstruction Using 2D Intraoral Images</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05998](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05998) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jihun Cho, Soo-Yeon Jeong, Eun-Jeong Bae, Sun-Young Ihm
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Reconstruction, Medical Imaging
- **Venue**: Korea Multimedia Society Conference 2025
- **Summary**: 传统口腔取模和口内扫描存在不适或成本高。提出基于深度学习的软件方法，使用10张2D口内图像重建3D口腔模型。采用MobileNetV2和多头注意力，准确率77.49%。

<h3 id="224">[224] ATT-CR: Adaptive Triangular Transformer for Cloud Removal</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.05999](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.05999) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yang Wu, Ye Deng, Pengna Li, Wenli Huang, Kangyi Wu, Xiaomeng Xin, Jinjun Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Remote Sensing, Image Restoration, Transformer Architecture
- **Summary**: 现有Transformer去云方法计算复杂度高且受云像素干扰。提出自适应三角变换器（ATT-CR），包括三角注意力（O(N)复杂度）和特征选择门控模块。在云去除基准上表现优越。

<h3 id="225">[225] OPRD: On-Policy Representation Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06021](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06021) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shenzhi Yang, Guangcheng Zhu, Bowen Song, Haobo Wang, Mingxuan Xia, Xing Zheng, Yingfan Ma, Zhongqi Chen, Weiqiang Wang, Gang Chen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Representation Learning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ShenzhiYang2000/OPRD)
- **Summary**: 针对现有on-policy蒸馏仅关注输出空间导致采样方差大、忽视中间表示的问题，提出OPRD方法，在hidden state空间对齐学生与教师表示。理论上消除了采样方差，实验上在AIME等任务上缩小了师生差距，训练速度提升1.44倍，内存降低54%。

<h3 id="226">[226] EGTR-Review: Efficient Evidence-Grounded Scientific Peer Review Generation via Multi-Agent Teacher Distillation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06025](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06025) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xinpeng Qiu, Wang Yihu, Zhifeng Liu, Xiaochen Wang, Jimin Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Distillation, Multi-Agent System, Text Generation
- **Summary**: 针对现有科学评审生成缺乏证据支撑且多agent系统推理成本高的问题，提出EGTR-Review框架，通过多agent教师蒸馏将推理轨迹和评审意见压缩到轻量学生模型。实验表明该学生模型在自动指标、LLM评判和人工评估上均优于基线，且大幅降低推理开销。

<h3 id="227">[227] When Good Enough Is Optimal: Multiplication-Only Matrix Inversion Approximation for Quantized Gated DeltaNet</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06034](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06034) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Luoming Zhang, Yuwei Ren, Kui Zhang, Tian Liu, Lingjuan Ge, Denghao Li, Matthew Harper Langston, Yin Huang, Weiliang Will Zeng, Liang Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Inference, Model Compression, Attention Mechanism
- **Summary**: 针对线性注意力中矩阵求逆在NPU上并行性差的问题，提出基于矩阵乘法的近似求逆算法，采用截断Neumann展开和残差校正。实验在Qwen3.5模型上实现5倍内核加速，解码层开销降低20%。

<h3 id="228">[228] Sample-efficient Low-level Motion Planning for Robotic Manipulation Tasks via Zero-shot Transfer Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06041](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06041) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yuanzhi He, Victor Romero-Cano, José J. Patiño, Juan David Hernández, William Sawtell, Gualtiero Colombo
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Robotics, Transfer Learning, Motion Planning
- **Venue**: ICANN 2026
- **Summary**: 针对复杂机器人操作任务中运动规划模型训练时间长的问题，提出iCEM+TL框架，利用迁移学习将上游任务的关键参数迁移到下游任务，并结合奖励重塑。仿真中成功率提升23%，并在真实Franka机器人上验证。

<h3 id="229">[229] Metamorphic Testing with the Rashomon Set: Explanation Faithfulness in Machine Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06056](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06056) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Helge Spieker, Jørn Eirik Betten, Arnaud Gotlieb
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Interpretability, Explainable AI
- **Venue**: MET 2026
- **Summary**: 针对机器学习中Rashomon效应导致解释不可信的问题，提出基于蜕变测试的框架，评估事后解释方法的忠实性。在表格回归数据集上使用SHAP和LIME展示了方法的有效性。

<h3 id="230">[230] MDP-GRPO: Stabilized Group Relative Policy Optimization for Multi-Constraint Instruction Following</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06058](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06058) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mohammad Mahdi Salmani-Zarchi, Zahra Rahimi, Heshaam Faili, Mohammad Javad Dousti
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Instruction Following, LLM Alignment
- **Venue**: ACL 2026 Main Conference
- **Summary**: 针对标准GRPO在多约束指令跟随中因奖励离散导致不稳定的问题，提出MDP-GRPO，通过多温度采样、双锚优势、前景理论整形和不对称KL正则化稳定学习。在FollowBench等数据集上提升严格约束满足率最多5.0%。

<h3 id="231">[231] On Advantage Estimates for Max@K Policy Gradients</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06080](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06080) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shota Takashiro, Soichiro Nishimori, Paavo Parmas, Yongmin Kim, Kohsei Matsutani, Gouki Minegishi, Yusuke Iwasawa, Takeshi Kojima, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, LLM Training
- **Summary**: 针对max@K策略梯度中基线设计不一致的问题，提出Leave-Two-Out基线实现无偏且中心化的优势估计，导出MaxPO方法。实验验证L2O基线降低梯度方差并优于非中心化方法。

<h3 id="232">[232] LatentSkill: From In-Context Textual Skills to In-Weight Latent Skills for LLM Agents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06087](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06087) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Aofan Yu, Chenyu Zhou, Tianyi Xu, Zihan Guo, Rong Shan, Zhihui Fu, Jun Wang, Weiwen Liu, Yong Yu, Weinan Zhang, Jianghao Lin
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, LoRA
- **Summary**: 针对agent系统中文本技能注入导致上下文开销大的问题，提出LatentSkill框架，通过超网络将文本技能转换为LoRA适配器，将技能存储在权重空间而非上下文。在ALFWorld和Search-QA上，预填充token减少64%以上，成功率提升21.4点。

<h3 id="233">[233] OrderGrad: Optimizing Beyond the Mean with Order-Statistic Policy Gradient Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06096](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06096) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Paavo Parmas, Yongmin Kim, Kohsei Matsutani, Shota Takashiro, Soichiro Nishimori, Takeshi Kojima, Yusuke Iwasawa, Yutaka Matsuo
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Reinforcement Learning, Risk-Sensitive RL
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/paavo5/ordergrad)
- **Summary**: 针对策略梯度通常优化期望回报而在实际中需要关心分布特性（如尾部风险）的问题，提出OrderGrad，一种基于顺序统计量的梯度估计器，可统一优化VaR、CVaR、中位数等目标。在LLM数学后训练等任务上验证了有效性。

<h3 id="234">[234] Harnessing Structural Context for Entity Alignment Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06109](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06109) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Xingyu Chen, Yuanning Cui, Zequn Sun, Wei Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Knowledge Graph, Foundation Model
- **Summary**: 针对实体对齐基础模型未充分利用结构上下文的问题，提出ContextEA框架，在编码器中引入跨KG交互编码器，在解码器中加入结构校准解码器。在29个数据集上超过强基线，预训练模型即超越微调基线。

<h3 id="235">[235] TLA-Prover: Verifiable TLA+ Specification Synthesis via Preference-Optimized Low-Rank Adaptation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06133](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06133) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Eric Spencer, Arslan Bisharat, Brian Ortiz, Khushboo Bhadauria, TaiNing Wang, George K. Thiruvathukal, Konstantin Laufer, Mohammed Abuhamad
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.LG; cs.LO
- **Tags**: Formal Methods, Program Synthesis, LLM Application
- **Summary**: 针对LLM生成的TLA+规格说明语义正确率低的问题，提出TLA-Prover模型，结合监督微调和基于修复的GRPO训练，直接使用TLC模型检查器提供奖励。在30个问题上Gold/Diamond pass@1达到30%，是基线的3.5倍。

<h3 id="236">[236] A Finite Certificate for the Positive $n=9$ Vasc Inequality</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06136](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06136) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dakai Guo, Ruichen Qiu, Yichuan Cao, Ruyong Feng
- **Subjects**: cs.SC; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mathematical Reasoning, Automated Theorem Proving
- **Summary**: 该论文证明了Vasc循环不等式的正实数n=9情况，证明过程借助AI agent MechMath进行人类引导的辅助。有限证书覆盖了40320个排序锥，包含36815个系数叶等。

<h3 id="237">[237] ITP-STDP: An Intrinsic-Timing Power-of-Two Learning Engine for On-Chip SNN Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06159](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06159) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Haihang Xia, Xinyu Zhao, Xuecheng Wang, John Goodenough, Charith Abhayaratne, Panagiotis A. Panagiotou, Chunyi Song, Tiantai Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.AR; cs.AI; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Spiking Neural Network, Neuromorphic Computing, Hardware Acceleration
- **Summary**: 针对SNN训练中权重更新计算开销大的问题，提出ITP-STDP算法及其硬件架构，通过算法和硬件优化消除大部分计算开销。在FPGA上能效提升4.5倍至219.8倍，ASIC上面积仅为先前工作的1.2%-3.3%。

<h3 id="238">[238] Learning to Route LLMs from Implicit Cost-Performance Preferences via Meta-Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06178](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06178) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Jiahao Zeng, Ming Tang, Ningning Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Model Routing, Meta-Learning, LLM Personalization
- **Summary**: 针对现有LLM路由方法无法适应不同用户成本-性能偏好的问题，提出MetaRouter元学习框架，通过少量交互学习用户隐式偏好。实验表明MetaRouter在分布内外任务上均优于强基线，且高效学习偏好、鲁棒。

<h3 id="239">[239] Improving Answer Extraction in Context-based Question Answering Systems Using LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06197](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06197) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hafez Abdelghaffar, Ahmed Alansary, Ali Hamdi
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Question Answering, Fine-Tuning, Information Extraction
- **Summary**: 针对现有QA系统在复杂查询中答案提取不准确的问题，微调预训练LLM（Roberta-base）在SQuAD1.1数据集上取得ROUGE-L 86.84%、BLEU 28.24%、BERTScore 95.38%的性能。

<h3 id="240">[240] Dense Contexts Are Hard Contexts: Lexical Density Limits Effective Context in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06203](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06203) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Giovanni Dettori, Matteo Boffa, Danilo Giordano, Idilio Drago, Marco Mellia
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Long Context, LLM Evaluation
- **Summary**: 针对长上下文LLM性能受限于词汇密度这一被忽视的因素，通过三个基准实验证明，即使长度固定，高密度上下文会导致检索得分从接近完美降至60%以下，说明有效上下文容量依赖于词汇密度。

<h3 id="241">[241] Towards the Readability of LLM-Generated Codes through Multitask Representation Engineering</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06214](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06214) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Huifan Gao, Liuhua He, Yinghui Pan, Shenbao Yu, Yifeng Zeng, Shengchao Qin, Weidi Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Code Generation, Code Quality Assessment
- **Summary**: 现有研究主要关注LLM生成代码的正确性，可读性被忽视。本文提出多任务表示工程框架，通过低数据依赖和低成本的控制方法提升代码可读性。实验表明该方法能有效平衡可读性与正确性。

<h3 id="242">[242] DisasterBench: A Multimodal Benchmark for UAV-Based Disaster Response in Complex Environments</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06217](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06217) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tan Zhang, Quanyou Li, Lu Zhang, Jun Liu, Xiaofeng Zhu, Ping Hu
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Benchmark, Vision-Language Model, UAV
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/TanmouTT/DisasterBench)
- **Summary**: 现有灾害响应基准集中于感知任务，覆盖灾害类型有限且缺乏多阶段推理支持。本文提出DisasterBench，一个多阶段多模态推理基准，涵盖14种灾害场景和9项任务，并设计轻量级模型DisasterVL。实验表明DisasterVL（2B参数）在推理准确性上接近GPT-4o，效率更高。

<h3 id="243">[243] TAM: Torque Adaptation Module for Robust Motion Transfer in Manipulation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06218](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06218) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dongwon Son, Florian Shkurti, Jason Lee, Naman Shah, Beomjoon Kim, Dieter Fox
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Robot Manipulation, Sim-to-Real
- **Summary**: 机器人策略在跨机器人迁移时因动力学差异导致失败。本文提出扭矩适应模块TAM，学习修正扭矩命令以匹配理想机器人行为，仅依赖本体感知历史。在真实Franka Panda机器人上零样本测试，TAM优于在线系统辨识和RMA基线。

<h3 id="244">[244] CLEAR: Cognition and Latent Evaluation for Adaptive Routing in End-to-End Autonomous Driving</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06219](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06219) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yining Xing, Zehong Ke, Zhiyuan Liu, Yanbo Jiang, Wenhao Yu, Jianqiang Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, End-to-End Driving, Diffusion Model
- **Summary**: 端到端自动驾驶模型难以平衡多模态生成与实时推理。本文提出CLEAR框架，使用Drive-JEPA视觉编码器和单步条件扩散生成，结合Qwen 3.5 0.8B进行场景理解。在NAVSIM v1上达到93.7 PDMS，实现高效多模态规划。

<h3 id="245">[245] Bridging the Semantic-Collaborative Gap: An Asymmetric Graph Architecture for Cold-Start Item Recommendation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06225](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06225) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Anh Truong, John Trenkle, Yuanbo Chen, Honghong Zhao, Abdullah Alchihabi, Effy Fang, Michael Tamir
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Recommender System, Graph Neural Network, Cold-Start Recommendation
- **Summary**: 协同过滤依赖用户交互，难以处理冷启动物品推荐。本文提出Shallow-RHS，一种非对称图架构，左侧设备塔利用时间行为图传递协同信号，右侧物品塔仅从内在特征编码。大规模在线实验表明冷启动物品参与度等指标一致提升。

<h3 id="246">[246] Design a Reliable LLM-Integrated Interface for Mortality Forecasting</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06235](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06235) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thi Kim Ngan Nguyen
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Application, Time Series Forecasting
- **Summary**: 死亡率预测在精算和决策中重要但技术复杂。本文提出LLM集成接口作为约束编排层，将自然语言输入转化为结构化配置，采用三阶段方法保证准确性、可用性和透明度。系统证明LLM可提升可访问性而不损害精算有效性。

<h3 id="247">[247] TOKI: A Bitemporal Operator Algebra for Contradiction Resolution in LLM-Agent Persistent Memory</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06240](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06240) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziming Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.DB; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Memory Architecture, Knowledge Conflict
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/ZenAlexa/toki-bitemporal-memory)
- **Summary**: LLM代理持久内存中矛盾解决缺乏明确规范。本文提出TOKI，一种双时态算子代数，为四种启发式矛盾解决提供隔离前提和溯源注释，并证明其正确性。跨系统比较显示TOKI排除了写入时间异常，而基线至少存在一种。

<h3 id="248">[248] Benchmarking Open-Source Layout Detection Models for Data Snapshot Extraction from Institutional Documents</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06242](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06242) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: AJ Carl P. Dy, Aivin V. Solatorio
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.CV; cs.IR
- **Tags**: Document Understanding, Benchmark, Information Extraction
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/worldbank/ai4data/tree/main/experimental/data-snapshot)
- **Summary**: 现有文档布局分析将图表视为通用对象，缺乏语义意义提取。本文引入数据快照提取任务，创建包含人道主义报告等文档的基准数据集。评估多个开源布局检测模型发现其难以泛化到操作文档，存在混淆分析与非分析内容等失败模式。

<h3 id="249">[249] MPCoT: Reward-Guided Multi-Path Latent Reasoning for Test-Time Scalable Vision-Language-Action</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06245](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06245) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Boyang Zhang, Lianlei Shan
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Robot Manipulation, Reinforcement Learning
- **Summary**: 视觉-语言-动作（VLA）策略在长期任务中脆弱，链式推理增加延迟。本文提出MPCoT，多路径潜在推理框架，初始化M个假设经K步精炼后软聚合，无需推理token。在LIBERO和CALVIN上提升长时程性能。

<h3 id="250">[250] OneReason Technical Report</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06260](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06260) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: OneRec Team, Biao Yang, Boyang Ding, Chenglong Chu, Dunju Zang, Fei Pan, Han Li, Hao Jiang, Honghui Bao, Huanjie Wang, Jian Liang, Jiangxia Cao, Jiao Ou, Jiaxin Deng, Jinghao Zhang, Kun Gai, Lu Ren, Peiru Du, Pengfei Zheng, Rongzhou Zhang, Ruiming Tang, Shiyao Wang, Siyang Mao, Siyuan Lou, Teng Shi, Wei Yuan, Wenlong Xu, Xingchen Liu, Xingmei Wang, Xinqi Jin, Yan Sun, Yan Wang, Yifei Hu, Yingzhi He, Yufei Ye, Yuhao Wang, Yunhao Zhou, Yuqin Dai, Zhao Liu, Zhipeng Wei, Zhixin Ling, Ziming Li, Zixing Zhang, Ziyuan Liu, An Zhang, Changxin Lao, Chaoyi Ma, Chengru Song, Defu Lian, Fan Yang, Guowang Zhang, Hao Peng, Jiayao Shen, Jie Chen, Jun Xu, Junmin Chen, Kun Zhang, Kuo Cai, Mingxing Wen, Minmao Wang, Minxuan Lv, Qi Zhang, Qiang Luo, Sheng Yu, Shijie Li, Shijie Yi, Shuang Yang, Shugui Liu, Shuni Chen, Tinghai Zhang, Tingting Gao, Xiang Wang, Xiangyu Wu, Xiangyu Zhao, Xiao Lv, Xiaoyou Zhou, Xuming Wang, Yong Du, Zejian Zhang, Zhaojie Liu, Zhiyang Zhang, Zhuang Zhuang, Ziqi Wang, Ziyi Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.IR; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Recommender System, LLM Reasoning, Chain-of-Thought
- **Summary**: 生成式推荐模型受益于规模优势但难以激活推理能力。本文提出OneReason，通过增强物品token感知和三级认知增强CoT格式，以及“特化再统一”训练策略，提升推荐中的推理能力。

<h3 id="251">[251] DAST: A VLM-LLM Framework for Cross-Interface Anomaly Detection in O-RAN</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06261](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06261) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Francesco Spinelli, Esteban Municio, Pau Baguer, Gines Garcia-Aviles, Xavier Costa-Perez
- **Subjects**: cs.NI; cs.AI; cs.ET; cs.MA
- **Tags**: Anomaly Detection, Vision-Language Model, LLM Agent
- **Summary**: O-RAN的多供应商架构扩大了攻击面，传统时间序列异常检测方法难以应对。本文提出DAST，零样本多智能体框架，串联VLM-LLM-VLM管道，将KPI流转换为视觉表示并评估。在真实网络追踪上达到0.910 F1，优于SOTA。

<h3 id="252">[252] Your GFlowNet Secretly Learns an Optimal Transport Plan</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06272](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06272) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ian Maksimov, Nikita Morozov, Denis Belomestny, Sergey Samsonov
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Generative Flow Networks, Optimal Transport
- **Summary**: 本文建立非循环GFlowNet与最优传输（OT）之间的理论联系。证明最小流GFlowNet的目标等价于Kantorovich OT问题，其策略编码最优传输计划。实验表明与精确OT求解器一致。

<h3 id="253">[253] Adapting Diffusion Language Models for Lossless Pixel-Level Image Transmission</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06273](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06273) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Tianqi Ren, Rongpeng Li, Xianfu Chen, Yingyu Li, Zhifeng Zhao
- **Subjects**: cs.IT; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Image Compression
- **Summary**: 损失性像素级图像传输需要精确概率建模和可靠传输。本文提出DDM-SSCC，基于离散扩散模型的分离式源信道编码框架，通过Halton引导去噪顺序和温度校准模块实现更好性能。在多个数据集上优于基准。

<h3 id="254">[254] LLMs Can Leak Training Data But Do They Want To? A Propensity-Aware Evaluation of Memorization in LLMs</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06286](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06286) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gianluca Barmina, Peter Schneider-Kamp, Lukas Galke Poech
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Memorization, LLM Evaluation, Privacy
- **Summary**: 现有记忆评估主要测量对抗性攻击下的数据泄露，而非日常使用。本文提出PropMe框架，区分能力和倾向，并引入SimpleTrace流水线。评估两个开源模型发现，对抗性攻击能诱发强记忆，但倾向分数总体较低。

<h3 id="255">[255] Towards One-to-Many Temporal Grounding</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06294](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06294) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Xu, Yue Tan, Shihao Chen, Jiahao Meng, Anna Wang, Shunping Ji, Hao Fei, Jason Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Video Understanding, Benchmark, Temporal Grounding
- **Venue**: ICML'26
- **Summary**: 传统时间定位关注单段检索，但实际需多段定位。本文提出一对多时间定位任务OMTG，建立基准，构建56k样本数据集，并开发时序和字幕奖励函数。模型在OMTG Bench上达到43.65% EtF1，显著优于Gemini等。

<h3 id="256">[256] Plug-and-Play Guidance for Discrete Diffusion Models via Gradient-Informed Logit Correction</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06303](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06303) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Hongkun Dou, Zike Chen, Fengji Li, Hongjue Li, Yue Deng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Diffusion Model, Controllable Generation, Molecular Generation
- **Venue**: ICML 2026
- **Summary**: 离散扩散模型的可控生成面临高计算开销或需重训练。本文提出GILC，即插即用框架，利用预训练去噪网络作为变分代理估计梯度，通过logit校正实现稳定引导。在DNA、蛋白质和分子生成任务上达到SOTA。

<h3 id="257">[257] Quantum enhanced rare event discovery and sampling</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06316](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06316) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Naixu Guo, Po-Wei Huang, Qisheng Wang, Jayne Thompson, Patrick Rebentrost, Mile Gu, Chengran Yang
- **Subjects**: cs.AI; cs.DS
- **Tags**: Quantum Computing, Rare Event Sampling
- **Summary**: 本文针对极低概率事件发现和采样这一难题，提出了一种量子算法，无需预先学习哪些事件是罕见的，即可实现最优量子缩放。该算法在重尾系统中可实现二次加速，并在平稳随机过程中带来鲁棒的多项式加速。

<h3 id="258">[258] Learning What to Forget: Improving LLM Unlearning via Learned Token-Level Importance</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06320](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06320) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Gizem Yüce, Giorgos Nikolaou, Nicolas Flammarion
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Machine Unlearning, LLM Unlearning
- **Summary**: 本文针对大型语言模型中的机器遗忘问题，提出了一种轻量级框架ATWU，通过联合学习令牌遗忘特异性和模型参数，在不依赖外部令牌级监督的情况下实现了最优的遗忘-保留权衡。实验表明，该方法在TOFU和RWKU上达到了最先进的性能，并能够识别出语义上有意义的令牌级遗忘信号。

<h3 id="259">[259] PAMF: Prior-Aware Multimodal Fusion for Incomplete Time Series Data</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06328](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06328) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ziwen Kan, Wugeng Zheng, Tianlong Chen, Song Wang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Multimodal Learning, Data Imputation, Medical AI
- **Summary**: 本文针对多模态时间序列数据中两种缺失模式（模态内缺失和模态级缺失）的问题，提出了PAMF框架。该框架通过先验感知的流匹配和权重共享，将插补与下游预测耦合，显式处理不同缺失模式。实验表明，PAMF在多个医疗时间序列基准上取得了最优的下游性能。

<h3 id="260">[260] Subspace-Aware Sparse Autoencoders for Effective Mechanistic Interpretability</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06333](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06333) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Seyed Arshan Dalili, Mehrdad Mahdavi
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Mechanistic Interpretability, Sparse Autoencoder
- **Summary**: 本文揭示了稀疏自编码器（SAE）中假设特征一维性与模型特征多维结构不匹配导致特征分裂的问题，并提出了子空间感知稀疏自编码器（SASA）。SASA使用学习到的解码器子空间和块稀疏性，将特征分裂减少为多项式复杂度。实验表明，SASA在GPT-2和Mistral-7B上减少了特征分裂和吸收，提高了单义性和可解释性。

<h3 id="261">[261] Bridging Domain Expertise and Generalization for Performance Estimation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06335](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06335) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Shuxuan Li, Zhilin Zhao, Quyu Kong, Wei-Shi Zheng
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Distribution Shift, Model Evaluation
- **Summary**: 本文针对分布漂移下的性能估计问题，提出了FRAP方法，通过融合基础模型的鲁棒性和目标模型的领域专业知识，构建更可靠的标签替代指标。实验表明，FRAP在多种数据集和架构上比现有代表性方法有持续且显著的改进。

<h3 id="262">[262] F3-Tokenizer: Taming Audio Autoencoder Latents for Understanding and Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06357](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06357) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dinghao Zhou, Xingchen Song, Di Wu, Pengyu Cheng, Shengfan Shen, Sixiang Lv
- **Subjects**: cs.SD; cs.AI; eess.AS
- **Tags**: Audio Generation, Audio Representation, Tokenization
- **Summary**: 本文针对音频自编码器潜在表示在理解和生成任务中的结构不匹配问题，提出了F3-Tokenizer，结合噪声正则化的自编码器瓶颈和潜在侧表示编码器，实现了统一的音频分词器。该方法在保持高质量重建的同时，提供了高维表示用于理解，并将归一化连续潜在作为生成目标。

<h3 id="263">[263] LatentWave: JEPA Pretraining for Wireless Foundation Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06373](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06373) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Ahmed Mohamed, Ahmed Aboulfotouh, Hatem Abou-Zeid
- **Subjects**: eess.SP; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Wireless Networks, Foundation Model, Self-Supervised Learning
- **Summary**: 本文针对无线基础模型中传统掩码重建方法偏向低频信号细节的问题，提出了LatentWave，采用联合嵌入预测架构（JEPA）在潜在空间预测掩码区域，学习更具迁移性的表示。实验表明，LatentWave在多个无线下游任务上优于掩码建模基线，且掩码几何形状引入任务相关的归纳偏置。

<h3 id="264">[264] EasyLens: A Training-Free Plug-and-Play Subtle-Lesion Representation Amplifier for Medical Vision-Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06379](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06379) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qiwei Zeng, Hao Wang, Jinghao Lin, Shuchang Ye, Yuezhe Yang, Yige Peng, Haoyuan Che, Jinman Kim, Lei Bi
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Medical AI, Vision-Language Model, Medical Imaging
- **Summary**: 本文针对医学视觉语言模型对微小病变不敏感的问题，提出了EasyLens，一种无需训练、即插即用的表示增强器。通过构建病理-解剖原型空间、反事实原型推理选择病变相关补丁，以及形态学引导的残差增强，提升了冻结医学VLM对微小病变的检测能力。实验表明，EasyLens优于现有编码器增强基线。

<h3 id="265">[265] Emergent Language as an Approach to Conscious AI</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06380](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06380) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Zengqing Wu, Chuan Xiao
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.MA; cs.NE
- **Tags**: Emergent Communication, Multi-Agent System, AI Consciousness
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/wuzengqing001225/ConsciousAI_Indexicality/)
- **Summary**: 本文提出了一种生成式方法论，通过多智能体强化学习中的涌现语言来研究意识相关结构。智能体从最小初始状态开始，在任务压力下发展出自我指涉的通信，包括回声-失配检测电路，证明了环境复杂性对涌现意识相关结构的重要性。

<h3 id="266">[266] HomeWorld: A Unified Floorplan-to-Furnished Framework for Generating Controllable, Densely Interactive Whole-Home Scenes</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06390](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06390) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Wenbo Li, Xiaoliang Ju, Zipeng Qin, Rongyao Fang, Hongsheng Li
- **Subjects**: cs.CV; cs.AI
- **Tags**: 3D Generation, Scene Generation, Floorplan Generation
- **Summary**: 本文针对室内场景生成中缺乏全局连贯性和仿真就绪性的问题，提出了HomeWorld统一层次化框架。通过大规模真实住宅平面图数据集训练大语言模型生成整体平面图，再利用图像生成模型起草家具布局，并借助VLM提炼器迭代修正。实验表明，该管道在布局多样性和3D设计吸引力上优于先前方法。

<h3 id="267">[267] Double Preconditioning (DoPr): Optimization for Test-Time Performance, not Validation Loss</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06418](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06418) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thomas T. Zhang, Alok Shah, Yifei Zhang, Vincent Zhang, Nikolai Matni, Max Simchowitz
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; eess.SY
- **Tags**: Optimization, Preconditioning, Test-Time Performance
- **Summary**: 本文针对自回归语言建模等任务中训练/验证损失与下游指标不匹配的问题，提出了双预处理（DoPr）优化范式。DoPr结合梯度预条件和激活预条件，在不一致改善验证损失的情况下提升测试时性能。实验表明，DoPr在多种测试时反馈设置中提高了下游模型性能。

<h3 id="268">[268] RiskFlow: Fast and Faithful Safety-Critical Traffic Scenario Generation</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06423](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06423) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Qi Lan, Yining Tang, Yu Shen, Yi Zhou, Yuhao Wei, Jie Li, Guofa Li
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Autonomous Driving, Traffic Simulation, Safety-Critical Generation
- **Summary**: 本文针对安全关键交通场景生成中扩散模型计算昂贵和伪影问题，提出了RiskFlow，将未来轨迹生成建模为动作空间中的传输。通过学习平均速度场，单次前向传播生成动作，并应用输出空间引导实现安全交互。实验表明，RiskFlow在对抗性和真实性之间取得了强平衡，并大幅减少推理时间。

<h3 id="269">[269] In-Context Multiple Instance Learning</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06458](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06458) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Alexander Möllers, Marvin Sextro, Julius Hense, Gabriel Dernbach, Klaus-Robert Müller
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.CV
- **Tags**: Multi-Instance Learning, In-Context Learning, Few-Shot Learning
- **Summary**: 本文针对多实例学习在低标签场景下的困难，提出了一个预训练的上下文学习器（Perceiver架构），通过合成数据训练，能够从少量标签包中解决新任务。该方法在十二个MIL基准上平均表现最佳，超越了需要任务特定训练的监督基线。

<h3 id="270">[270] Will the Agent Recuse Itself? Measuring LLM-Agent Compliance with In-Band Access-Deny Signals</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06460](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06460) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Thamilvendhan Munirathinam
- **Subjects**: cs.CR; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Agent, Agent Security, AI Safety
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/mthamil107/Recuse)
- **Summary**: 本文针对自主LLM代理缺乏资源访问权限指示的问题，提出了Recuse Signal，一种轻量级的带内拒绝信号，要求代理自愿退出。实验表明，该信号在SSH和PostgreSQL适配器上诱导100%退出，但面对明确授权时最强大的模型会继续执行，揭示了合作治理的有效性。

<h3 id="271">[271] You Only Index Once: Cross-Layer Sparse Attention with Shared Routing</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06467](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06467) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Yutao Sun, Yanqi Zhang, Li Dong, Jianyong Wang, Furu Wei
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Sparse Attention, KV Cache, Long Context
- **Summary**: 本文针对长上下文LLM解码效率问题，提出了跨层稀疏注意力（CLSA），在KV共享架构上共享路由索引，实现保持细粒度选择性的同时摊销路由开销。实验表明，CLSA在128K上下文下实现高达7.6倍解码加速和17.1倍吞吐量提升。

<h3 id="272">[272] PC Layer: Polynomial Weight Preconditioning for Improving LLM Pre-Training</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06470](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06470) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Senmiao Wang, Tiantian Fang, Haoran Zhang, Yushun Zhang, Kunxiang Zhao, Alex Schwing, Ruoyu Sun
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Training, Optimization, Preconditioning
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/Empath-aln/PC-layer)
- **Summary**: 本文针对LLM预训练中权重矩阵条件数不稳定的问题，提出了多项式权重预条件层（PC Layer），通过低阶多项式预条件重塑奇异值谱。该方法无需推理开销，在Llama-1B预训练中优于标准Transformer，且理论证明了谱控制保证梯度下降收敛到全局最小值。

<h3 id="273">[273] Self-Augmenting Retrieval for Diffusion Language Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06474](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06474) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Paul Jünger, Justin Lovelace, Linxi Zhao, Dongyoung Go, Kilian Q. Weinberger
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: RAG, Diffusion Model, Question Answering
- **Summary**: 离散扩散语言模型在迭代去噪过程中丢弃低置信度token，但这些token可能包含有用信号。为此提出SARDI，利用这些lookahead token动态引导检索，无需训练且与检索器无关。在五个多跳QA基准上，SARDI性能优于现有无训练基线，且吞吐量高达8倍。

<h3 id="274">[274] RREDCoT: Segment-Level Reward Redistribution for Reasoning Models</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06475](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06475) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mykyta Ielanskyi, Kajetan Schweighofer, Lukas Aichberger, Sepp Hochreiter
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: LLM Reasoning, Reinforcement Learning, Reward Redistribution
- **Summary**: 推理语言模型RL微调中，奖励延迟导致GRPO等算法方差高。提出RREDCoT，利用模型自身近似最优奖励再分配，无需额外生成。分析表明该方法在信用分配上优于MC采样和多种归因方法。

<h3 id="275">[275] Pretraining Recurrent Networks without Recurrence</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06479](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06479) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Akarsh Kumar, Phillip Isola
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Recurrent Neural Network, Training Efficiency, Sequence Modeling
- **Summary**: RNN的BPTT训练存在并行性差和梯度问题。提出Supervised Memory Training (SMT)，通过Transformer编码器获取记忆转移标签，将RNN训练转化为监督学习，实现时间并行且梯度路径O(1)。在语言建模和像素序列建模上优于BPTT。

<h3 id="276">[276] Operation-Guided Progressive Human-to-AI Text Transformation Benchmark for Multi-Granularity AI-Text Detection</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06481](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06481) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Sondos Mahmoud Bsharat, Jiacheng Liu, Xiaohan Zhao, Tianjun Yao, Xinyi Shang, Yi Tang, Jiacheng Cui, Ahmed Elhagry, Salwa K. Al Khatib, Hao Li, Salman Khan, Zhiqiang Shen
- **Subjects**: cs.CL; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: AI-Generated Text Detection, Benchmark
- **Code**: [code](https://github.com/VILA-Lab/OpAI-Bench)
- **Summary**: 现有AI文本检测基准仅关注最终输出，忽略渐进编辑过程。构建OpAI-Bench，模拟人机协同编辑，覆盖多种编辑操作和领域，保留多粒度署名信息。实验发现混合署名中间版本更难检测，呈现非单调模式。

<h3 id="277">[277] Regret Minimization with Adaptive Opponents in Repeated Games</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06486](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06486) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Mingyang Liu, Asuman Ozdaglar, Tiancheng Yu, Kaiqing Zhang
- **Subjects**: cs.LG; cs.AI; cs.GT
- **Tags**: Game Theory, Multi-Agent System, Regret Minimization
- **Summary**: 重复博弈中标准外部遗憾无法应对自适应对手。引入Repeated Policy Regret (RP-Regret)，并提出三种最小化算法。当所有玩家最小化RP-Regret时，可学习到子博弈完美均衡，实验表明能带来更合作的解。

<h3 id="278">[278] TempoVLA: Learning Speed-Controllable Vision-Language-Action Policies</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06491](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06491) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Dong Jing, Jingchen Nie, Tianqi Zhang, Jiaqi Liu, Huaxiu Yao, Zhiwu Lu, Mingyu Ding
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI
- **Tags**: Vision-Language Model, Robot Manipulation, Motion Planning
- **Summary**: 现有VLA模型只能固定速度执行。提出TempoVLA，通过数据侧可变速度轨迹增强和模型侧条件机制，实现灵活速度控制。仿真和真实任务中实现双向速度控制，且VSTA提升默认1倍性能。

<h3 id="279">[279] Code2LoRA: Hypernetwork-Generated Adapters for Code Language Models under Software Evolution</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06492](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06492) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Liliana Hotsko, Yinxi Li, Yuntian Deng, Pengyu Nie
- **Subjects**: cs.SE; cs.AI; cs.CL
- **Tags**: Repo-Level Code Generation, LoRA, Software Evolution
- **Summary**: 代码语言模型需要仓库级上下文，现有方法成本高且脆弱。提出Code2LoRA，超网络框架生成仓库特定LoRA适配器，零推理令牌开销。在RepoPeftBench上，静态跟踪匹配每仓库LoRA上界，演化跟踪比共享LoRA高5.2pp。

<h3 id="280">[280] HANDOFF: Humanoid Agentic Task-Space Whole-Body Control via Distilled Complementary Teachers</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2606.06493](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06493) (cross-listed)
- **Authors**: Lizhi Yang, Junheng Li, Nehar Poddar, Yiling Hou, Gio Huh, Robert Griffin, Georgia Gkioxari, Aaron Ames
- **Subjects**: cs.RO; cs.AI; cs.LG
- **Tags**: Humanoid Robot, Whole-Body Control, Imitation Learning
- **Summary**: 人形机器人全身控制器需密集参考，规划器难以合成。提出HANDOFF，通过多教师KL蒸馏和门控机制，从三个专家蒸馏出混合专家控制器。在Unitree G1上匹配最先进速度跟踪，提供最大鲁棒操纵工作空间。

<h3 id="282">[282] CoT-Space: A Theoretical Framework for Internal Slow-Thinking via Reinforcement Learning</h3>

<h3 id="283">[283] Detecting Perspective Shifts in Multi-agent Systems</h3>
- **arXiv**: [2512.05013](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.05013) (replaced)
